==============================================================================
BALE <-> TELEGRAM CONTROLLER PWA — COMPLETE SOURCE (v0.3.0)
Generated: 2026-09-18T14:22:09.379Z
Features: PWA dashboard, Bale bot remote controller, Telegram
MTProto (GramJS) user session stored encrypted in a private
channel, auto-pair, simple login, /receive file fetcher,
auto-forward of ALL private chats, inline-button relay, clock,
backup/restore, production standalone deploy pipeline.
==============================================================================

HOW TO RUN
------------------------------
1) bun install
2) bun run build        # produces .next/standalone (+ static, public, .env)
3) bash .zscripts/dev.sh   # or: NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 \
      BRIDGE_DATA_DIR=/path/.data node .next/standalone/server.js
4) Open http://localhost:3000 (SIMPLE_LOGIN=1 -> zero-click panel)
5) Send ANY message to the Bale bot -> auto-paired as owner

##############################################################################
### FILE: .zscripts/dev.sh
##############################################################################
#!/bin/bash

set -euo pipefail

# 获取脚本所在目录（.zscripts）
# 使用 $0 获取脚本路径（与 build.sh 保持一致）
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"

log_step_start() {
        local step_name="$1"
        echo "=========================================="
        echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] Starting: $step_name"
        echo "=========================================="
        export STEP_START_TIME
        STEP_START_TIME=$(date +%s)
}

log_step_end() {
        local step_name="${1:-Unknown step}"
        local end_time
        end_time=$(date +%s)
        local duration=$((end_time - STEP_START_TIME))
        echo "=========================================="
        echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] Completed: $step_name"
        echo "[LOG] Step: $step_name | Duration: ${duration}s"
        echo "=========================================="
        echo ""
}

start_mini_services() {
        local mini_services_dir="$PROJECT_DIR/mini-services"
        local started_count=0

        log_step_start "Starting mini-services"
        if [ ! -d "$mini_services_dir" ]; then
                echo "Mini-services directory not found, skipping..."
                log_step_end "Starting mini-services"
                return 0
        fi

        echo "Found mini-services directory, scanning for sub-services..."

        for service_dir in "$mini_services_dir"/*; do
                if [ ! -d "$service_dir" ]; then
                        continue
                fi

                local service_name
                service_name=$(basename "$service_dir")
                echo "Checking service: $service_name"

                if [ ! -f "$service_dir/package.json" ]; then
                        echo "[$service_name] No package.json found, skipping..."
                        continue
                fi

                if ! grep -q '"dev"' "$service_dir/package.json"; then
                        echo "[$service_name] No dev script found, skipping..."
                        continue
                fi

                echo "Starting $service_name in background..."
                (
                        cd "$service_dir"
                        echo "[$service_name] Installing dependencies..."
                        bun install
                        echo "[$service_name] Running bun run dev..."
                        exec bun run dev
                ) >"$PROJECT_DIR/.zscripts/mini-service-${service_name}.log" 2>&1 &

                local service_pid=$!
                echo "[$service_name] Started in background (PID: $service_pid)"
                echo "[$service_name] Log: $PROJECT_DIR/.zscripts/mini-service-${service_name}.log"
                disown "$service_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
                started_count=$((started_count + 1))
        done

        echo "Mini-services startup completed. Started $started_count service(s)."
        log_step_end "Starting mini-services"
}

wait_for_service() {
        local host="$1"
        local port="$2"
        local service_name="$3"
        local max_attempts="${4:-60}"
        local attempt=1

        echo "Waiting for $service_name to be ready on $host:$port..."

        while [ "$attempt" -le "$max_attempts" ]; do
                if curl -s --connect-timeout 2 --max-time 5 "http://$host:$port" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
                        echo "$service_name is ready!"
                        return 0
                fi

                echo "Attempt $attempt/$max_attempts: $service_name not ready yet, waiting..."
                sleep 1
                attempt=$((attempt + 1))
        done

        echo "ERROR: $service_name failed to start within $max_attempts seconds"
        return 1
}

cleanup() {
        if [ -n "${DEV_PID:-}" ] && kill -0 "$DEV_PID" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
                echo "Stopping Next.js dev server (PID: $DEV_PID)..."
                kill "$DEV_PID" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
        fi
}

trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM

cd "$PROJECT_DIR"

if ! command -v bun >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo "ERROR: bun is not installed or not in PATH"
        exit 1
fi

log_step_start "bun install"
echo "[BUN] Installing dependencies..."
bun install
log_step_end "bun install"

log_step_start "bun run db:push"
echo "[BUN] Setting up database..."
bun run db:push
log_step_end "bun run db:push"

log_step_start "Starting Next.js server"
if [ ! -f ".next/standalone/server.js" ]; then
    echo "[NODE] standalone build missing — running bun run build..."
    bun run build
fi
echo "[NODE] Starting production standalone server (single process — required for bridge state sharing)..."
export BRIDGE_DATA_DIR="$PROJECT_DIR/.data"
NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0 node .next/standalone/server.js 2>&1 | tee dev.log &
DEV_PID=$!
log_step_end "Starting Next.js server"

log_step_start "Waiting for Next.js server"
wait_for_service "localhost" "3000" "Next.js server"
log_step_end "Waiting for Next.js server"

log_step_start "Health check"
echo "[NODE] Performing health check..."
curl -fsS localhost:3000 >/dev/null
echo "[NODE] Health check passed"
log_step_end "Health check"

start_mini_services

echo "Next.js dev server is running in background (PID: $DEV_PID)."
echo "Use 'kill $DEV_PID' to stop it."
disown "$DEV_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
unset DEV_PID

##############################################################################
### FILE: Caddyfile
##############################################################################
:81 {
	@transform_port_query {
		query XTransformPort=*
	}

	handle @transform_port_query {
		reverse_proxy localhost:{query.XTransformPort} {
			header_up Host {host}
			header_up X-Forwarded-For {remote_host}
			header_up X-Forwarded-Proto {scheme}
			header_up X-Real-IP {remote_host}
		}
	}

	handle {
		reverse_proxy localhost:3000 {
			header_up Host {host}
			header_up X-Forwarded-For {remote_host}
			header_up X-Forwarded-Proto {scheme}
			header_up X-Real-IP {remote_host}
		}
	}
}

// This is your Prisma schema file,
// learn more about it in the docs: https://pris.ly/d/prisma-schema

// Looking for ways to speed up your queries, or scale easily with your serverless or edge functions?
// Try Prisma Accelerate: https://pris.ly/cli/accelerate-init

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "sqlite"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

model User {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  email     String   @unique
  name      String?
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model Post {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  title     String
  content   String?
  published Boolean  @default(false)
  authorId  String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

      "src": "/icons/icon-512.png",
      "sizes": "512x512",
      "type": "image/png",

    },
    {
      "src": "/icons/icon-512-maskable.png",
      "sizes": "512x512",
      "type": "image/png",
      "purpose": "maskable"
    }
  ]
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: public/sw.js
##############################################################################
/*
  Service worker — PWA shell caching with hard security rules:
  - Never caches /api/* responses (auth, sessions, messages).
  - Never caches POST requests.
  - Navigation falls back to offline.html when the server is down.
*/

const CACHE = "bridge-shell-v2";
const PUBLIC_FILES = [
  "/offline.html",
  "/manifest.webmanifest",
  "/icons/icon-192.png",
  "/icons/icon-512.png",
  "/icons/icon-512-maskable.png",
];

self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .open(CACHE)
      .then((cache) => cache.addAll(PUBLIC_FILES))
      .then(() => self.skipWaiting())
  );
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .keys()
      .then((keys) =>
        Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE).map((k) => caches.delete(k)))
      )
      .then(() => self.clients.claim())
  );
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  const request = event.request;
  const url = new URL(request.url);

  if (request.method !== "GET" || url.origin !== self.location.origin) return;

  // Authentication, status, sessions and messages must never be cached.
  if (url.pathname.startsWith("/api/")) return;

  if (request.mode === "navigate") {
    event.respondWith(
      fetch(request).catch(() => caches.match("/offline.html"))
    );
    return;
  }

  if (PUBLIC_FILES.includes(url.pathname)) {
    event.respondWith(
      caches.match(request).then((cached) => cached || fetch(request))
    );
  }
});

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/app/api/auth/route.ts
##############################################################################
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  createAuthCookie,
  checkPassword,
  tooManyAuthFailures,
  registerAuthFailure,
  originAllowed,
} from "@/lib/auth";
import { CONFIG } from "@/lib/bridge/env";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!originAllowed(req)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid request origin." }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));

  /*
    SIMPLE_LOGIN mode: one click opens the panel — no password.
    Otherwise keep the rate-limited password check.
  */
  if (!CONFIG.SIMPLE_LOGIN) {
    if (tooManyAuthFailures()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "برای مدتی کوشش نکنید." }, { status: 429 });
    }

    if (!checkPassword(body?.password)) {
      registerAuthFailure();
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "رمز پنل نادرست است." }, { status: 401 });
    }
  }

  const response = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  response.headers.set("Set-Cookie", createAuthCookie(req));
  return response;
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticated, originAllowed, clearAuthCookie } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!authenticated(req) || !originAllowed(req)) {
    const response = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    response.headers.set("Set-Cookie", clearAuthCookie());
    return response;
  }

  const response = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  response.headers.set("Set-Cookie", clearAuthCookie());
  return response;
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/app/api/send-file/route.ts
##############################################################################
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticated, originAllowed, unauthorized } from "@/lib/auth";
import { sendFile, formatResults } from "@/lib/bridge/telegram";
import { MAX_BYTES } from "@/lib/bridge/env";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!authenticated(req)) return unauthorized();
  if (!originAllowed(req)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid request origin." }, { status: 403 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const encoded = String(body?.base64 || "");
    const maxEncoded = Math.ceil(MAX_BYTES / 3) * 4 + 4;

    if (!encoded || encoded.length > maxEncoded) {
      throw new Error("حجم فایل نامعتبر است.");
    }

    const buffer = Buffer.from(encoded, "base64");
    const result = await sendFile(
      body?.targets,
      buffer,
      String(body?.filename || "file.bin"),
      String(body?.caption || "")
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ result: formatResults(result) });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: (error as Error)?.message || "خطای نامشخص" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/app/api/telegram/probe/route.ts
##############################################################################
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticated, unauthorized } from "@/lib/auth";
import {
  analyzeMedia,
  extMatches,
  fetchChannelFile,
  resolvePeer,
  telegram,
} from "@/lib/bridge/telegram";
import { bot, safeFilename } from "@/lib/bridge/botapi";
import { getSettings } from "@/lib/bridge/settings";

/*
  Diagnostic endpoint for /receive: lists the media found in the
  last messages of a chat (@username or numeric id) together with
  every candidate extension, and shows which of them would match
  the wanted extension with which x-N index.

  mode=fetch additionally runs the REAL fetchChannelFile pipeline
  (peer resolution + hidden-history join + scan + download) and,
  with send=1, delivers the file to the Bale owner exactly like
  the /receive command does.
*/
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!authenticated(req)) return unauthorized();

  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const target = String(url.searchParams.get("target") || "").trim();
  const limit = Math.min(
    50,
    Math.max(1, Number(url.searchParams.get("limit")) || 30)
  );
  const rawExt = String(url.searchParams.get("ext") || "").trim();
  const mode = String(url.searchParams.get("mode") || "");

  if (!target) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "target is required (@username or id)" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }

  try {
    if (mode === "fetch") {
      const index = Math.max(0, Number(url.searchParams.get("index")) || 0);
      const ext = rawExt || "*";
      const found = await fetchChannelFile(target, ext, index);

      let delivered: unknown = "skipped";
      if (url.searchParams.get("send") === "1") {
        const ownerId = getSettings().ownerId;
        await bot(
          "bale",
          "sendDocument",
          {
            chat_id: ownerId,
            caption: Array.from(
              "🧪 تست دریافت از: " + found.source +
                " | پیام: " + found.msgId +
                "\nنام: " + found.name +
                " | حجم: " + (found.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2) + " MB"
            ).slice(0, 900).join(""),
          },
          {
            buffer: found.buffer,
            name: safeFilename(found.name),
            mime: found.mime,
          }
        );
        delivered = "sent to owner";
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        mode: "fetch",
        target,
        name: found.name,
        mime: found.mime,
        size: found.size,
        msgId: found.msgId,
        source: found.source,
        joined: found.joined || false,
        delivered,
      });
    }

    const result = await telegram(async (client) => {
      const peer = await resolvePeer(client, target);
      const messages = await client.getMessages(peer as never, { limit });

      const wanted = rawExt && !rawExt.startsWith(".") ? "." + rawExt : rawExt;
      const rows: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
      let matchIndex = 0;

      for (const message of messages) {
        const info = analyzeMedia(message);
        if (!info) continue;

        const matched = wanted ? extMatches(wanted, info.exts) : false;

        rows.push({
          msgId: message.id,
          kind: info.kind,
          mime: info.mime || null,
          name: info.name || null,
          exts: info.exts,
          sizeMB: Number((info.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)),
          matched,
          matchIndex: matched ? matchIndex++ : null,
        });
      }

      let title = target;
      try {
        const entity = (await client.getEntity(peer as never)) as unknown as {
          title?: string;
          username?: string;
        };
        title =
          entity.title || (entity.username ? "@" + entity.username : target);
      } catch {
        /* keep raw target */
      }

      return {
        target,
        title,
        fetched: messages.length,
        mediaCount: rows.length,
        wanted: wanted || null,
        matches: matchIndex,
        rows,
      };
    });

    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: (error as Error)?.message || "خطای نامشخص" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/app/api/tg/begin/route.ts
##############################################################################
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticated, originAllowed, unauthorized } from "@/lib/auth";
import { beginLogin } from "@/lib/bridge/telegram";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!authenticated(req)) return unauthorized();
  if (!originAllowed(req)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid request origin." }, { status: 403 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const phone = String(body?.phone || "").replace(/\s/g, "");
    if (!/^\+\d{8,15}$/.test(phone)) {
      throw new Error("شماره را با فرمت جهانی وارد کنید؛ مثلا +98912...");
    }
    return NextResponse.json(await beginLogin(phone));
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: (error as Error)?.message || "خطای نامشخص" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }
}

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import "./globals.css";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "پل بله و تلگرام",
  description:
    "کنترل حساب تلگرام از طریق ربات بله — پنل PWA با پشتیبان‌گیری سشن روی کانال خصوصی",
  manifest: "/manifest.webmanifest",
  icons: {
    icon: "/icons/icon-192.png",
    apple: "/icons/icon-192.png",
  },
  appleWebApp: {
    capable: true,
    statusBarStyle: "default",
    title: "پل پیام",
  },
};

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  themeColor: "#0d9488",
  width: "device-width",
  initialScale: 1,
  maximumScale: 1,
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="fa" dir="rtl" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <link
          rel="stylesheet"
          href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/rastikerdar/vazirmatn@v33.003/Vazirmatn-font-face.css"

      <body className="antialiased bg-background text-foreground min-h-screen flex flex-col">
        {children}
        <Toaster />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/app/page.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  KeyRound,
  Radio,
  Activity,
  LogIn,
  Bot,
  Users,
  Send,
  Settings2,
  ScrollText,
  Info,
  Download,
} from "lucide-react";
import { api, Status } from "@/components/dashboard/shared";
import { StatusTab } from "@/components/dashboard/status-tab";
import { TelegramLogin } from "@/components/dashboard/telegram-login";
import { BalePair } from "@/components/dashboard/bale-pair";
import { Dialogs } from "@/components/dashboard/dialogs";
import { SendPanel } from "@/components/dashboard/send";
import { SettingsForm } from "@/components/dashboard/settings-form";
import { LogsView } from "@/components/dashboard/logs-view";

type Notice = { message: string; ok: boolean; id: number } | null;

type InstallPromptEvent = Event & {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: string }>;
};

const DEFAULT_PHONE_PLACEHOLDER = "";

export default function Home() {
  const [authorized, setAuthorized] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [simpleLogin, setSimpleLogin] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<Notice>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [targets, setTargets] = useState("");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const [defaultPhone, setDefaultPhone] = useState(DEFAULT_PHONE_PLACEHOLDER);
  const [installEvent, setInstallEvent] = useState<InstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const noticeTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const showNotice = useCallback((message: string, ok: boolean) => {
    setNotice({ message, ok, id: Date.now() });
    if (noticeTimer.current) clearTimeout(noticeTimer.current);
    noticeTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setNotice(null), 7000);
  }, []);

  const refresh = useCallback(
    async (initial = false) => {
      try {
        const s = await api<Status>("/api/status");
        setStatus(s);
        setAuthorized(true);
      } catch {
        // 401 → show the login gate; other errors keep state.
        setAuthorized(false);
      }
      if (initial) {
        // warm-up of public config happens in the boot effect
      }
    },
    []
  );

  /* Boot: register SW, fetch phone hint, auto-login, check auth. */
  useEffect(() => {
    if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
      navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js").catch(() => {});
    }

    (async () => {
      let simple = false;
      try {
        const d = await fetch("/api/config-public", { cache: "no-store" })
          .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null));
        if (d?.defaultPhone) setDefaultPhone(d.defaultPhone);
        if (d?.simpleLogin) {
          simple = true;
          setSimpleLogin(true);
        }
      } catch {}

      /*
        Opening the site IS opening the panel: in simple mode the
        auth cookie is issued automatically — no button, no form.
      */
      if (simple) {
        try {
          await api("/api/auth", {});
        } catch {}
      }

      await refresh(true);
    })();
  }, [refresh]);

  /* Periodic status polling while authorized. */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authorized) return;
    const timer = setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        setStatus(await api<Status>("/api/status"));
      } catch {
        // Cookie expired: silently re-enter in simple mode.
        if (simpleLogin) {
          try {
            await api("/api/auth", {});
            setStatus(await api<Status>("/api/status"));
            return;
          } catch {}
        }
        setAuthorized(false);
      }
    }, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [authorized, simpleLogin]);

  /* PWA install prompt. */
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (event: Event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      setInstallEvent(event as InstallPromptEvent);
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", handler);
  }, []);

  async function login() {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await api("/api/auth", { password });
      setPassword("");
      await refresh(true);
      showNotice("ورود به پنل موفق بود.", true);
    } catch (error) {
      showNotice((error as Error).message, false);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function logout() {
    try {
      await api("/api/logout", {});
    } catch {}
    setAuthorized(false);
    setStatus(null);
  }

  function pickDialog(id: string) {
    setTargets((prev) => {
      const list = prev
        .split(",")
        .map((x) => x.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
      if (!list.includes(id)) list.push(id);
      return list.join(",");
    });
  }

  /* ---------- login gate ---------- */
  if (authorized === false) {
    return (
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <Card className="w-full max-w-sm">
          <CardHeader className="text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto mb-2 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-primary/10">
              <Radio className="h-7 w-7 text-primary" />
            </div>
            <CardTitle>پل بله و تلگرام</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>
              {simpleLogin
                ? "ورود ساده — رمز لازم نیست. فقط دکمه را بزنید تا پنل باز شود."
                : "کنترل حساب تلگرام از طریق ربات بله. برای باز کردن پنل،\n              رمز مدیریت را وارد کنید."}
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            {simpleLogin ? (
              <Button
                onClick={login}
                disabled={busy}
                className="h-12 w-full text-base"
              >
                <LogIn className="ml-1 h-5 w-5" />
                ورود به پنل
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <form
                className="space-y-3"
                onSubmit={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  login();
                }}
              >
                <Input
                  type="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="رمز پنل مدیریت"
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  required
                />
                <Button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="w-full">
                  <KeyRound className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
                  ورود به پنل
                </Button>
              </form>
            )}
            {notice && (
              <p
                className={
                  "mt-3 rounded-md p-2 text-sm " +
                  (notice.ok
                    ? "bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 dark:bg-teal-950 dark:text-teal-200"
                    : "bg-red-50 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-950 dark:text-red-300")
                }
              >
                {notice.message}
              </p>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </main>
    );
  }

  /* ---------- loading ---------- */
  if (!authorized) {
    return (
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">در حال بررسی وضعیت…</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  /* ---------- dashboard ---------- */
  return (
    <main className="flex-1 w-full max-w-4xl mx-auto p-4 pb-10">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2 mb-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary/10">
            <Radio className="h-6 w-6 text-primary" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-lg font-bold leading-tight">پل بله و تلگرام</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              کنترل حساب شخصی تلگرام با ربات بله — سشن روی کانال خصوصی
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {installEvent && (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={() => {
                installEvent.prompt().finally(() => setInstallEvent(null));
              }}
            >
              <Download className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              نصب برنامه
            </Button>
          )}
          <Badge
            variant="outline"
            className={
              status?.connected
                ? "border-teal-600 text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-400"
                : "border-amber-500 text-amber-600"
            }
          >
            {status?.connected ? "متصل" : "قطع / در حال اتصال"}
          </Badge>
        </div>
      </header>

      {notice && (
        <div
          role="status"
          className={
            "mb-4 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap " +
            (notice.ok
              ? "bg-teal-50 text-teal-900 dark:bg-teal-950 dark:text-teal-200"
              : "bg-red-50 text-red-800 dark:bg-red-950 dark:text-red-200")
          }
        >
          {notice.message}
        </div>
      )}

      <Tabs defaultValue="status" dir="rtl" className="w-full">
        <TabsList className="w-full justify-start overflow-x-auto h-auto flex-wrap">
          <TabsTrigger value="status" className="gap-1">
            <Activity className="h-4 w-4" />
            وضعیت
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="login" className="gap-1">
            <LogIn className="h-4 w-4" />
            ورود تلگرام
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="bale" className="gap-1">
            <Bot className="h-4 w-4" />
            بله
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="dialogs" className="gap-1">
            <Users className="h-4 w-4" />
            گفتگوها
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="send" className="gap-1">
            <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
            ارسال
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="settings" className="gap-1">
            <Settings2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            تنظیمات
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="logs" className="gap-1">
            <ScrollText className="h-4 w-4" />
            گزارش‌ها
          </TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>

        <TabsContent value="status" className="mt-4">
          <StatusTab
            status={status}
            busy={busy}
            setBusy={setBusy}
            onNotice={showNotice}
            onChanged={() => {
              refresh();
              setRefreshKey((k) => k + 1);
            }}
            onLogout={logout}
          />
          <Card className="mt-4">
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base">
                <Info className="h-4 w-4" />
                نکات عملیاتی
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="text-sm text-muted-foreground space-y-1.5">
              <p>• فقط یک نمونه از این برنامه با همین سشن و توکن بله اجرا کنید.</p>
              <p>• دریافت پیام‌های بله با Polling است؛ اگر Webhook دیگری تنظیم شده باشد حذف کنید.</p>
              <p>• رمز پنل، کلید رمزنگاری و توکن‌ها را منتشر نکنید.</p>
              <p>• تغییر مکرر نام و بیو ممکن است باعث FloodWait شود.</p>
              <p>• خاموش‌شدن (Sleep) میزبان همه کارهای پس‌زمینه را متوقف می‌کند.</p>
              <p dir="ltr" className="text-left text-xs">
                Bale bot: @gttttt55yu_bot
              </p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="login" className="mt-4">
          <TelegramLogin
            defaultPhone={defaultPhone}
            busy={busy}
            setBusy={setBusy}
            onDone={() => refresh()}
            onNotice={showNotice}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="bale" className="mt-4">
          <BalePair
            ownerId={status?.ownerId || ""}
            busy={busy}
            setBusy={setBusy}
            onChanged={() => refresh()}
            onNotice={showNotice}
          />
          <Card className="mt-4">
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-base">دستورات ربات بله</CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>
                پس از اتصال مالک، این دستورها را در چت خصوصی ربات بفرستید:
              </CardDescription>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div dir="ltr" className="rounded-md bg-muted p-3 font-mono text-xs space-y-0.5 text-left">
                <p>/id — نمایش شناسه شما</p>
                <p>/status — وضعیت کامل پل</p>
                <p>/dialogs — فهرست گفتگوهای اخیر</p>
                <p>/to &lt;targets&gt; — تنظیم مقصدهای ارسال</p>
                <p>/send &lt;targets&gt; &lt;text&gt; — ارسال پیام</p>
                <p>/watch &lt;ids&gt; | * | off — دریافت خودکار از تلگرام</p>
                <p>/clock off|name|bio|both — ساعت روی پروفایل</p>
                <p>/backup — پشتیبان‌گیری سشن روی کانال</p>
                <p>/reconnect — اتصال مجدد</p>
                <p>/pause — توقف پل</p>
                <p>/resume — ادامه کار پل</p>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                پیام متنی یا فایل معمولی که برای ربات بفرستید، به
                مقصدهای ذخیره‌شده در «/to» منتقل می‌شود.
              </p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="dialogs" className="mt-4">
          <Dialogs
            busy={busy}
            setBusy={setBusy}
            onNotice={showNotice}
            onPick={pickDialog}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="send" className="mt-4">
          <SendPanel
            status={status}
            busy={busy}
            setBusy={setBusy}
            targets={targets}
            setTargets={setTargets}
            onNotice={showNotice}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="settings" className="mt-4">
          <SettingsForm
            status={status}
            busy={busy}
            setBusy={setBusy}
            onNotice={showNotice}
            onSaved={() => refresh()}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="logs" className="mt-4">
          <LogsView refreshKey={refreshKey} />
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>

      <footer className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)]">
        هیچ سشن یا پیام خصوصی در کش آفلاین مرورگر ذخیره نمی‌شود.
      </footer>
    </main>
  );
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/components/dashboard/bale-pair.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Link2, Unlink, Copy, Bot, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import { api } from "./shared";

type PairingInfo = {
  command: string;
  expiresAt: number;
  open: boolean;
};

export function BalePair({
  ownerId,
  busy,
  setBusy,
  onChanged,
  onNotice,
}: {
  ownerId: string;
  busy: boolean;
  setBusy: (v: boolean) => void;
  onChanged: () => void;
  onNotice: (msg: string, ok: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [pairing, setPairing] = useState<PairingInfo | null>(null);

  async function run(fn: () => Promise<void>) {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function startPairing(open: boolean) {
    return run(async () => {
      const result = await api<PairingInfo>("/api/pair", { open });
      setPairing(result);
      onNotice(
        open
          ? "اتصال سریع فعال شد — حالا در بله به ربات پیام بدهید."
          : "کد ۶ رقمی ساخته شد — در بله برای ربات بفرستید.",
        true
      );
    });
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Bot className="h-5 w-5" />
          اتصال مالک بله
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          ساده‌ترین راه: همین حالا در بله به ربات هر پیامی بفرستید —
          اولین حسابی که پیام بدهد بلافاصله مالک ربات می‌شود. دکمه‌های
          زیر فقط گزینه‌های پشتیبان‌اند.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-3">
        {ownerId ? (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Badge variant="secondary" className="font-mono" dir="ltr">
              Owner ID: {ownerId}
            </Badge>
            <Button
              variant="destructive"
              size="sm"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={() =>
                run(async () => {
                  await api("/api/unpair", {});
                  setPairing(null);
                  onChanged();
                  onNotice("اتصال مالک بله حذف شد.", true);
                })
              }
            >
              <Unlink className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              حذف اتصال
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : pairing ? (
          pairing.open ? (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="rounded-lg border border-teal-600/40 bg-teal-50 p-3 text-sm text-teal-900 dark:bg-teal-950 dark:text-teal-200">
                اتصال سریع فعال شد.
                <br />
                ۱) در بله، چت خصوصی ربات را باز کنید.
                <br />
                ۲) هر پیامی (مثلاً «سلام» یا /start) بفرستید.
                <br />
                ۳) حساب شما بلافاصله مالک ربات می‌شود.
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                تا ۵ دقیقه اعتبار دارد. اولین حسابی که پیام بدهد مالک می‌شود.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <code
                  dir="ltr"
                  className="rounded bg-muted px-3 py-2 font-mono text-lg font-bold tracking-widest select-all"
                >
                  {pairing.command}
                </code>
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => {
                    navigator.clipboard
                      ?.writeText(pairing.command)
                      .then(() => onNotice("دستور کپی شد.", true))
                      .catch(() => {});
                  }}
                >
                  <Copy className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
                  کپی
                </Button>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                تا ۵ دقیقه اعتبار دارد. این دستور را در چت خصوصی ربات بله
                بفرستید تا حساب شما به‌عنوان تنها مالک ثبت شود.
              </p>
            </div>
          )
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button disabled={busy} onClick={() => startPairing(true)}>
              <Zap className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              اتصال سریع (بدون کد)
            </Button>
            <Button variant="outline" disabled={busy} onClick={() => startPairing(false)}>
              <Link2 className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              ساخت کد ۶ رقمی
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

### FILE: src/components/dashboard/dialogs.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
import { Users, RefreshCw, Copy, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { api, DialogRow } from "./shared";

export function Dialogs({
  busy,
  setBusy,
  onNotice,
  onPick,
}: {
  busy: boolean;
  setBusy: (v: boolean) => void;
  onNotice: (msg: string, ok: boolean) => void;
  onPick: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const [dialogs, setDialogs] = useState<DialogRow[]>([]);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return dialogs;
    return dialogs.filter(
      (d) =>
        d.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        d.username.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        d.id.includes(q)
    );
  }, [dialogs, query]);

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Users className="h-5 w-5" />
          گفتگوها
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          ۱۰۰ گفتگوی اخیر حساب تلگرام برای یافتن شناسه عددی مقصدها.
          روی هر گفتگو بزنید تا شناسه آن به فیلد مقصد اضافه شود.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() => {
              setBusy(true);
              api<DialogRow[]>("/api/dialogs")
                .then((rows) => {
                  setDialogs(rows);
                  onNotice(rows.length + " گفتگو دریافت شد.", true);
                })
                .catch((error) => onNotice(error.message, false))
                .finally(() => setBusy(false));
            }}
          >
            <RefreshCw className={"ml-1 h-4 w-4" + (busy ? " animate-spin" : "")} />
            دریافت گفتگوها
          </Button>
          <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[180px]">
            <Search className="absolute right-2 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="جست‌وجوی عنوان، شناسه یا @یوزرنیم"
              className="pr-8"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <ScrollArea className="h-72 rounded-md border">
          <div className="p-2 text-sm" dir="rtl">
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="p-4 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                {dialogs.length === 0
                  ? "هنوز گفتگویی دریافت نشده است."
                  : "نتیجه‌ای یافت نشد."}
              </p>
            ) : (
              filtered.map((d) => (
                <button
                  key={d.id}
                  className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded px-2 py-2 text-right hover:bg-accent"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onPick(d.id);
                    onNotice("شناسه به فیلد مقصد اضافه شد: " + d.id, true);
                  }}
                >
                  <span className="truncate">
                    {d.title || "بدون عنوان"}
                    {d.username ? (
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground" dir="ltr">
                        {" "}
                        @{d.username}
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                    <code dir="ltr" className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {d.id}
                    </code>
                    <Copy className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground" />
                  </span>
                </button>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </ScrollArea>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/components/dashboard/logs-view.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
import { ScrollText, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { api, LogRow } from "./shared";

export function LogsView({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<LogRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    api<{ rows: LogRow[] }>("/api/logs")
      .then((data) => setRows(data.rows || []))
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  /* Load logs when the tab mounts or a refresh is requested. */
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    api<{ rows: LogRow[] }>("/api/logs")
      .then((data) => {
        if (!cancelled) setRows(data.rows || []);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [refreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      api<{ rows: LogRow[] }>("/api/logs")
        .then((data) => setRows(data.rows || []))
        .catch(() => {});
    }, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ScrollText className="h-5 w-5" />
          گزارش رویدادها
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          ۱۲۰ رویداد اخیر سرور. توکن‌ها و رمزها پیش از نمایش حذف
          (Redact) می‌شوند.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-3">
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={load} disabled={loading}>
          <RefreshCw className={"ml-1 h-4 w-4" + (loading ? " animate-spin" : "")} />
          بازخوانی
        </Button>
        <ScrollArea className="h-80 rounded-md border" dir="ltr">
          <div className="p-3 font-mono text-xs space-y-1">
            {rows.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-center text-muted-foreground">گزارشی نیست.</p>
            ) : (
              rows.map((row, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="flex gap-2">
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground shrink-0">
                    {row.time?.slice(11, 19)}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={
                      row.level === "error"
                        ? "text-red-500 font-semibold shrink-0"
                        : "text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 shrink-0"
                    }
                  >
                    {row.level === "error" ? "ERROR" : "info"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="break-all">{row.message}</span>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </ScrollArea>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/components/dashboard/send.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Send, Paperclip } from "lucide-react";
import { Status } from "./shared";

export function SendPanel({
  status,
  busy,
  setBusy,
  targets,
  setTargets,
  onNotice,
}: {
  status: Status | null;
  busy: boolean;
  setBusy: (v: boolean) => void;
  targets: string;
  setTargets: (v: string) => void;
  onNotice: (msg: string, ok: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const fileInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  async function run(fn: () => Promise<void>) {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const maxMB = status?.maxFileMB || 20;

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Send className="h-5 w-5" />
          ارسال پیام یا فایل به تلگرام
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          حداکثر ۱۰ مقصد، جداشده با کاما. متن می‌تواند HTML ساده مثل
          &lt;b&gt;متن&lt;/b&gt; داشته باشد. فایل‌ها برای حفظ فرمت به‌صورت
          Document ارسال می‌شوند (سقف {maxMB} مگابایت).
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-3">
        <Input
          dir="ltr"
          className="text-left"
          value={targets}
          onChange={(e) => setTargets(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="@channel1,-1001234567890"
        />

        <Textarea
          rows={4}
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="متن پیام یا کپشن فایل..."
        />

        <Input
          ref={fileInput}
          type="file"
          onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)}
          className="file:ml-2"
        />

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() =>
              run(async () => {
                const result = await fetch("/api/send", {
                  method: "POST",
                  credentials: "same-origin",
                  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                  body: JSON.stringify({ targets, text }),
                });
                const data = await result.json();
                if (!result.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
                onNotice(String(data.result || "ارسال شد."), true);
              })
            }
          >
            <Send className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
            ارسال متن
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="outline"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() =>
              run(async () => {
                if (!file) throw new Error("ابتدا فایل انتخاب کنید.");
                if (file.size > maxMB * 1024 * 1024) {
                  throw new Error("حجم فایل از سقف مجاز بیشتر است.");
                }

                const base64 = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
                  const reader = new FileReader();
                  reader.onerror = () =>
                    reject(new Error("خواندن فایل ناموفق بود."));
                  reader.onload = () =>
                    resolve(String(reader.result).split(",")[1] || "");
                  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                const result = await fetch("/api/send-file", {
                  method: "POST",
                  credentials: "same-origin",
                  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                  body: JSON.stringify({
                    targets,
                    filename: file.name,
                    base64,
                    caption: text,
                  }),
                });
                const data = await result.json();
                if (!result.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
                onNotice(String(data.result || "فایل ارسال شد."), true);
              })
            }
          >
            <Paperclip className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
            ارسال فایل با کپشن
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

### FILE: src/components/dashboard/settings-form.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Save, Settings2 } from "lucide-react";
import { api, Status } from "./shared";

export function SettingsForm({
  status,
  busy,
  setBusy,
  onNotice,
  onSaved,
}: {
  status: Status | null;
  busy: boolean;
  setBusy: (v: boolean) => void;
  onNotice: (msg: string, ok: boolean) => void;
  onSaved: () => void;
}) {
  const [sendTo, setSendTo] = useState("");
  const [watch, setWatch] = useState("");
  const [autoAll, setAutoAll] = useState(true);
  const [receiveText, setReceiveText] = useState(true);
  const [receiveFiles, setReceiveFiles] = useState(true);
  const [clockMode, setClockMode] = useState("off");
  const [clockInterval, setClockInterval] = useState("300");

  // Seed the form from status whenever it arrives/changes.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!status) return;
    setSendTo((status.sendTo || []).join(","));
    setWatch((status.watch || []).join(","));
    setAutoAll(status.autoAll !== false);
    setReceiveText(status.receiveText !== false);
    setReceiveFiles(status.receiveFiles !== false);
    setClockMode(status.clock || "off");
    setClockInterval(String(status.clockInterval || 300));
  }, [status]);

  async function save() {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await api("/api/settings", {
        sendTo,
        watch,
        autoAll,
        receiveText,
        receiveFiles,
        clock: {
          mode: clockMode,
          intervalSeconds: Number(clockInterval) || 300,
        },
      });
      onNotice("تنظیمات ذخیره شد.", true);
      onSaved();
    } catch (error) {
      onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Settings2 className="h-5 w-5" />
          تنظیمات پل
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          <b>دریافت خودکار همه چت‌های خصوصی (autoAll):</b> متن، فایل، فیلم و
          دکمه‌های ربات‌ها از هر شخصی به‌صورت خودکار به بات بله می‌رسد.
          <br />
          <b>دریافت از گروه/کانال (watch):</b> شناسه گفتگوهایی که پیام‌شان به
          بله می‌رسد؛ <code dir="ltr">*</code> یعنی همه گفتگوها.
          <br />
          <b>مقصدهای ارسال:</b> مقصدهای پیش‌فرض پیام و فایل از بله.
          <br />
          <b>ساعت روی پروفایل:</b> افزودن ساعت تهران به نام و/یا بیو حساب.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="sendTo">مقصدهای ارسال (sendTo)</Label>
          <Input
            id="sendTo"
            dir="ltr"
            className="text-left"
            value={sendTo}
            onChange={(e) => setSendTo(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="@channel1,-1001234567890"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="watch">دریافت از گروه/کانال تلگرام (watch)</Label>
          <Input
            id="watch"
            dir="ltr"
            className="text-left"
            value={watch}
            onChange={(e) => setWatch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="-1001234567890,123456789 یا * یا خالی=خاموش"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Switch
              id="autoAll"
              checked={autoAll}
              onCheckedChange={setAutoAll}
            />
            <Label htmlFor="autoAll">همه چت‌های خصوصی به بله (autoAll)</Label>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Switch
              id="receiveText"
              checked={receiveText}
              onCheckedChange={setReceiveText}
            />
            <Label htmlFor="receiveText">انتقال متن‌ها به بله</Label>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Switch
              id="receiveFiles"
              checked={receiveFiles}
              onCheckedChange={setReceiveFiles}
            />
            <Label htmlFor="receiveFiles">انتقال فایل‌ها به بله</Label>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>حالت ساعت پروفایل</Label>
            <Select value={clockMode} onValueChange={setClockMode}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-44">
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="off">خاموش</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="name">روی نام</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="bio">روی بیو</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="both">نام و بیو</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="interval">فاصله (ثانیه، حداقل ۶۰)</Label>
            <Input
              id="interval"
              dir="ltr"
              className="text-left w-28"
              type="number"
              min={60}
              value={clockInterval}
              onChange={(e) => setClockInterval(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <Button disabled={busy} onClick={save}>
          <Save className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
          ذخیره تنظیمات
        </Button>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

### FILE: src/components/dashboard/shared.ts
##############################################################################
"use client";

export type Status = {
  connected: boolean;
  phase: string;
  loggingIn: boolean;
  needsLogin: boolean;
  paused: boolean;
  connectionBlocked: boolean;
  ownerId: string;
  sendTo: string[];
  watch: string[];
  autoAll: boolean;
  receiveText: boolean;
  receiveFiles: boolean;
  clock: string;
  clockInterval: number;
  floodUntil: number;
  droppedEvents: number;
  queued: number;
  baleOffset: number;
  uptimeSeconds: number;
  maxFileMB: number;
};

export type DialogRow = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  username: string;
};

export type SettingsData = {
  ownerId: string;
  sendTo: string[];
  watch: string[];
  autoAll: boolean;
  receiveText: boolean;
  receiveFiles: boolean;
  clock: {
    mode: string;
    intervalSeconds: number;
  };
};

export type LogRow = {
  time: string;
  level: string;
  message: string;
};

export async function api<T>(
  url: string,
  body?: unknown
): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: body === undefined ? "GET" : "POST",
    credentials: "same-origin",
    cache: "no-store",
    headers: body === undefined ? {} : { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });

  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "درخواست ناموفق بود.");
  return data as T;
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/components/dashboard/status-tab.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  Activity,
  RefreshCw,
  CloudUpload,
  CloudDownload,
  Pause,
  Play,
  ShieldAlert,
} from "lucide-react";
import { api, Status } from "./shared";

const PHASE_LABELS: Record<string, { label: string; tone: "ok" | "warn" | "bad" }> = {
  connected: { label: "متصل به تلگرام", tone: "ok" },
  connecting: { label: "در حال اتصال…", tone: "warn" },
  "needs-login": { label: "نیازمند ورود", tone: "warn" },
  offline: { label: "قطع", tone: "bad" },
  paused: { label: "متوقف", tone: "warn" },
};

function PhaseBadge({ status }: { status: Status | null }) {
  if (!status) return <Badge variant="outline">…</Badge>;
  const phase = PHASE_LABELS[status.phase] || {
    label: status.phase,
    tone: "warn" as const,
  };
  const cls =
    phase.tone === "ok"
      ? "bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-600 text-white"
      : phase.tone === "bad"
        ? "bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-600 text-white"
        : "bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white";
  return <Badge className={cls}>{phase.label}</Badge>;
}

function Row({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-dashed py-1.5 last:border-0">
      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">{label}</span>
      <span className="text-sm font-medium" dir="auto">
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function fmtTime(ts: number): string {
  if (!ts || ts < Date.now()) return "—";
  const sec = Math.ceil((ts - Date.now()) / 1000);
  const min = Math.floor(sec / 60);
  return min > 0 ? `${min} دقیقه` : `${sec} ثانیه`;
}

export function StatusTab({
  status,
  busy,
  setBusy,
  onNotice,
  onChanged,
  onLogout,
}: {
  status: Status | null;
  busy: boolean;
  setBusy: (v: boolean) => void;
  onNotice: (msg: string, ok: boolean) => void;
  onChanged: () => void;
  onLogout: () => void;
}) {
  async function run(url: string, successMsg: string) {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await api(url, {});
      onNotice(successMsg, true);
      onChanged();
    } catch (error) {
      onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const upH = Math.floor((status?.uptimeSeconds || 0) / 3600);
  const upM = Math.floor(((status?.uptimeSeconds || 0) % 3600) / 60);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Activity className="h-5 w-5" />
            وضعیت پل
            <PhaseBadge status={status} />
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            سشن تلگرام فقط از روی فایل سنجاق‌شده در کانال خصوصی بازیابی
            می‌شود؛ هیچ نسخه محلی معتبری وجود ندارد.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <Row label="مالک بله" value={status?.ownerId ? status.ownerId : "متصل نشده"} />
            <Row label="مقصدهای ارسال" value={status?.sendTo?.length ? status.sendTo.join(" ، ") : "—"} />
            <Row
              label="دریافت خودکار"
              value={
                status?.watch?.length
                  ? status.watch.includes("*")
                    ? "همه گفتگوها (*)"
                    : status.watch.join(" ، ")
                  : "خاموش"
              }
            />
            <Row
              label="ساعت پروفایل"
              value={
                { off: "خاموش", name: "نام", bio: "بیو", both: "نام + بیو" }[
                  status?.clock || "off"
                ] || "—"
              }
            />
            <Row
              label="FloodWait"
              value={status?.floodUntil && status.floodUntil > Date.now() ? fmtTime(status.floodUntil) : "غیرفعال"}
            />
            <Row label="صف عملیات" value={String(status?.queued ?? 0)} />
            <Row label="رویدادهای دورریخته" value={String(status?.droppedEvents ?? 0)} />
            <Row label="آپ‌تایم" value={`${upH} ساعت و ${upM} دقیقه`} />
          </div>

          {status?.connectionBlocked && (
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950 p-2 text-sm text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">
              <ShieldAlert className="h-4 w-4" />
              وضعیت اتصال نامطمئن است؛ برنامه را از پنل ری‌استارت کنید.
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button disabled={busy} onClick={() => run("/api/tg/reconnect", "دستور اتصال مجدد اجرا شد.")}>
              <RefreshCw className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              اتصال مجدد
            </Button>
            <Button disabled={busy} variant="outline" onClick={() => run("/api/tg/backup", "سشن رمزگذاری و روی کانال سنجاق شد.")}>
              <CloudUpload className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              پشتیبان‌گیری فوری
            </Button>
            <Button disabled={busy} variant="outline" onClick={() => run("/api/tg/restore", "بازیابی از کانال انجام شد.")}>
              <CloudDownload className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              بازیابی از کانال
            </Button>
            {status?.paused ? (
              <Button disabled={busy} onClick={() => run("/api/resume", "پل ادامه یافت.")}>
                <Play className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
                ادامه کار پل
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button disabled={busy} variant="outline" onClick={() => run("/api/pause", "پل متوقف شد.")}>
                <Pause className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
                توقف پل
              </Button>
            )}
            <Button disabled={busy} variant="secondary" onClick={onLogout}>
              خروج از پنل
            </Button>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            خروج از پنل، اتصال حساب تلگرام یا ربات بله را قطع نمی‌کند.
          </p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/components/dashboard/telegram-login.tsx
##############################################################################
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { LogIn, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { api } from "./shared";

type Step = "phone" | "code" | "password";

export function TelegramLogin({
  defaultPhone,
  busy,
  setBusy,
  onDone,
  onNotice,
}: {
  defaultPhone: string;
  busy: boolean;
  setBusy: (v: boolean) => void;
  onDone: () => void;
  onNotice: (msg: string, ok: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("phone");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(defaultPhone);
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [twoFA, setTwoFA] = useState("");

  async function run(fn: () => Promise<void>) {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    setPhone(defaultPhone);
  }, [defaultPhone]);

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <LogIn className="h-5 w-5" />
          ورود تلگرام
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          شروع ورود جدید، اتصال جاری برنامه را می‌بندد. کد و رمز
          دومرحله‌ای هرگز ذخیره نمی‌شوند. پس از ورود موفق، سشن
          رمزگذاری‌شده روی کانال خصوصی پشتیبان‌گیری و سنجاق می‌شود.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-3">
        {step === "phone" && (
          <form
            className="space-y-3"
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              run(async () => {
                const result = await api<{ next: string }>("/api/tg/begin", {
                  phone,
                });
                setStep(result.next === "code" ? "code" : "phone");
                onNotice("کد تلگرام ارسال شد.", true);
              });
            }}
          >
            <Input
              dir="ltr"
              className="text-left"
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="+98912..."
              autoComplete="tel"
              required
            />
            <Button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="w-full sm:w-auto">
              ارسال کد
            </Button>
          </form>
        )}

        {step === "code" && (
          <form
            className="space-y-3"
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              run(async () => {
                try {
                  const result = await api<{ next?: string }>("/api/tg/code", {
                    code,
                  });
                  setCode("");
                  if (result?.next === "password") {
                    setStep("password");
                    onNotice("حساب شما رمز دومرحله‌ای دارد.", true);
                  } else {
                    setStep("phone");
                    onDone();
                    onNotice("ورود تلگرام موفق بود؛ سشن روی کانال پشتیبان شد.", true);
                  }
                } catch (error) {
                  onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
                }
              });
            }}
          >
            <Input
              dir="ltr"
              className="text-left tracking-widest"
              value={code}
              onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="کد ۵ رقمی تلگرام"
              autoComplete="one-time-code"
              required
            />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="flex-1 sm:flex-none">
                تأیید کد
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                disabled={busy}
                onClick={() => run(async () => {
                  await api("/api/tg/cancel", {});
                  setStep("phone");
                  setCode("");
                  setTwoFA("");
                })}
              >
                لغو ورود
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        {step === "password" && (
          <form
            className="space-y-3"
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              run(async () => {
                try {
                  await api("/api/tg/password", { password: twoFA });
                  setTwoFA("");
                  setStep("phone");
                  onDone();
                  onNotice("ورود تلگرام موفق بود؛ سشن روی کانال پشتیبان شد.", true);
                } catch (error) {
                  onNotice((error as Error).message, false);
                } finally {
                  setTwoFA("");
                }
              });
            }}
          >
            <Input
              dir="ltr"
              className="text-left"
              type="password"
              value={twoFA}
              onChange={(e) => setTwoFA(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="رمز دومرحله‌ای تلگرام"
              autoComplete="off"
              required
            />
            <Button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="w-full sm:w-auto">
              تأیید رمز
            </Button>
          </form>
        )}

        {step !== "phone" && (
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1">
            <XCircle className="h-3 w-3" />
            مرحله {step === "code" ? "۲ از ۳ — کد" : "۳ از ۳ — رمز دومرحله‌ای"}
            <Badge variant="outline" className="mr-1">
              {step}
            </Badge>
          </p>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as AccordionPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-accordion"
import { ChevronDownIcon } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

function Accordion({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <AccordionPrimitive.Root data-slot="accordion" {...props} />
}

function AccordionItem({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Item>) {
  return (
    <AccordionPrimitive.Item
      data-slot="accordion-item"
      className={cn("border-b last:border-b-0", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AccordionTrigger({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return (
    <AccordionPrimitive.Header className="flex">
      <AccordionPrimitive.Trigger
        data-slot="accordion-trigger"
        className={cn(
          "focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 flex flex-1 items-start justify-between gap-4 rounded-md py-4 text-left text-sm font-medium transition-all outline-none hover:underline focus-visible:ring-[3px] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&[data-state=open]>svg]:rotate-180",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
        <ChevronDownIcon className="text-muted-foreground pointer-events-none size-4 shrink-0 translate-y-0.5 transition-transform duration-200" />
      </AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>
    </AccordionPrimitive.Header>
  )
}

function AccordionContent({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Content>) {
  return (
    <AccordionPrimitive.Content
      data-slot="accordion-content"
      className="data-[state=closed]:animate-accordion-up data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-down overflow-hidden text-sm"
      {...props}
    >
      <div className={cn("pt-0 pb-4", className)}>{children}</div>
    </AccordionPrimitive.Content>
  )
}

export { Accordion, AccordionItem, AccordionTrigger, AccordionContent }

}

### FILE: src/components/ui/alert.tsx
##############################################################################
import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const alertVariants = cva(
  "relative w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm grid has-[>svg]:grid-cols-[calc(var(--spacing)*4)_1fr] grid-cols-[0_1fr] has-[>svg]:gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 items-start [&>svg]:size-4 [&>svg]:translate-y-0.5 [&>svg]:text-current",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-card text-card-foreground",
        destructive:
          "text-destructive bg-card [&>svg]:text-current *:data-[slot=alert-description]:text-destructive/90",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Alert({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert"
      role="alert"
      className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-title"
      className={cn(
        "col-start-2 line-clamp-1 min-h-4 font-medium tracking-tight",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-muted-foreground col-start-2 grid justify-items-start gap-1 text-sm [&_p]:leading-relaxed",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription }

### FILE: src/components/ui/badge.tsx
##############################################################################
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium w-fit whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 [&>svg]:size-3 gap-1 [&>svg]:pointer-events-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:ring-[3px] aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 aria-invalid:border-destructive transition-[color,box-shadow] overflow-hidden",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border-transparent bg-primary text-primary-foreground [a&]:hover:bg-primary/90",
        secondary:
          "border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground [a&]:hover:bg-secondary/90",
        destructive:
          "border-transparent bg-destructive text-white [a&]:hover:bg-destructive/90 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40 dark:bg-destructive/60",
        outline:
          "text-foreground [a&]:hover:bg-accent [a&]:hover:text-accent-foreground",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Badge({
  className,
  variant,
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span"> &
  VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> & { asChild?: boolean }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "span"

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="badge"
      className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants }

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/components/ui/breadcrumb.tsx
##############################################################################
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { ChevronRight, MoreHorizontal } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

function Breadcrumb({ ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"nav">) {
  return <nav aria-label="breadcrumb" data-slot="breadcrumb" {...props} />
}

function BreadcrumbList({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"ol">) {
  return (
    <ol
      data-slot="breadcrumb-list"
      className={cn(
        "text-muted-foreground flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm break-words sm:gap-2.5",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbItem({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"li">) {
  return (
    <li
      data-slot="breadcrumb-item"
      className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1.5", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbLink({
  asChild,
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"a"> & {
  asChild?: boolean
}) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "a"

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="breadcrumb-link"
      className={cn("hover:text-foreground transition-colors", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbPage({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="breadcrumb-page"
      role="link"
      aria-disabled="true"
      aria-current="page"
      className={cn("text-foreground font-normal", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbSeparator({
  children,
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"li">) {
  return (
    <li
      data-slot="breadcrumb-separator"
      role="presentation"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cn("[&>svg]:size-3.5", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children ?? <ChevronRight />}
    </li>
  )
}

function BreadcrumbEllipsis({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="breadcrumb-ellipsis"
      role="presentation"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cn("flex size-9 items-center justify-center", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      <MoreHorizontal className="size-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">More</span>
    </span>
  )
}

  Breadcrumb,
  BreadcrumbList,
  BreadcrumbItem,
  BreadcrumbLink,
  BreadcrumbPage,
  BreadcrumbSeparator,
  BreadcrumbEllipsis,
}

### FILE: src/components/ui/button.tsx
