/*
  Next.js instrumentation hook: starts the bridge exactly once per
  server process — Bale long-polling, Telegram connection manager
  and the maintenance loop.
*/

export async function register() {
  if (process.env.NEXT_RUNTIME !== "nodejs") return;

  try {
    const { ensureBridge } = await import("./lib/bridge/index");
    ensureBridge();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[bridge] failed to start:",
      (error as Error)?.message || error
    );
  }
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/auth.ts
##############################################################################
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { CONFIG, KEY } from "./bridge/env";
import { state } from "./bridge/state";
/*
  Dashboard authentication: signed HttpOnly cookie, timing-safe
  comparison, origin check for mutating requests and a simple
  failure-rate limit. Nothing sensitive is stored browser-side.

  Cookie SameSite strategy:
  - HTTPS (proxy or COOKIE_SECURE=1) -> SameSite=None; Secure so the
    dashboard also works inside cross-site preview iframes.
  - Plain HTTP (local testing)       -> SameSite=Lax.
*/

export const COOKIE_NAME = "bridge_auth";
const TWELVE_HOURS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function sign(value: string): string {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", KEY).update(value).digest("base64url");
}

export function createAuthCookie(req: NextRequest): string {
  const payload = Buffer.from(
    JSON.stringify({
      exp: Date.now() + TWELVE_HOURS,
      nonce: crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex"),
    })
  ).toString("base64url");

  const value = payload + "." + sign(payload);
  const https =
    CONFIG.COOKIE_SECURE ||
    req.headers.get("x-forwarded-proto") === "https";

  const sameSite = https ? "None" : "Lax";

  return (
    COOKIE_NAME + "=" + value +
    "; HttpOnly; SameSite=" + sameSite + "; Path=/" +
    "; Max-Age=" + Math.floor(TWELVE_HOURS / 1000) +
    (https ? "; Secure" : "")
  );
}

export function clearAuthCookie(): string {
  return (
    COOKIE_NAME + "=; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=0"
  );
}

export function authenticated(req: NextRequest): boolean {
  try {
    const raw = req.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value || "";
    const [payload, signature] = raw.split(".");
    if (!payload || !signature) return false;

    const expected = Buffer.from(sign(payload));
    const received = Buffer.from(signature);
    if (
      expected.length !== received.length ||
      !crypto.timingSafeEqual(expected, received)
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    const data = JSON.parse(
      Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString()
    );
    return data.exp > Date.now();
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/* Simple sliding window rate limit for password attempts. */
export function tooManyAuthFailures(): boolean {
  const s = state();
  if (Date.now() - s.authWindow > 15 * 60 * 1000) {
    s.authWindow = Date.now();
    s.authFailures = 0;
  }
  return s.authFailures >= 15;
}

export function registerAuthFailure(): void {
  state().authFailures++;
}

export function checkPassword(password: unknown): boolean {
  const hash = (s: string) =>
    crypto.createHash("sha256").update(s).digest();

  const provided = hash(String(password || ""));
  const expected = hash(CONFIG.ADMIN_PASSWORD);

  return (
    provided.length === expected.length &&
    crypto.timingSafeEqual(provided, expected)
  );
}

/*
  Cross-site protection for mutating API calls. Works behind the
  preview proxy by also accepting x-forwarded-host.

  In SIMPLE_LOGIN sandbox mode there is no password to protect, and
  preview proxies/iframes produce foreign Origin headers — so the
  origin check is skipped there.
*/
export function originAllowed(req: NextRequest): boolean {
  if (CONFIG.SIMPLE_LOGIN) return true;

  const origin = req.headers.get("origin");
  if (!origin) return true;

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(origin);
    const host =
      req.headers.get("x-forwarded-host") || req.headers.get("host");
    return parsed.host === host;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function unauthorized(): NextResponse {
  return NextResponse.json(
    { error: "ورود به پنل لازم است." },
    { status: 401 }
  );
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/actions.ts
##############################################################################
import { state } from "./state";
import { saveRuntime } from "./settings";
import { closeOldConnection, connectSaved, telegram } from "./telegram";
import { uploadBackup, readChannelBackup } from "./session";
import { log } from "./store";

/*
  Cross-cutting bridge actions shared by the API layer and the
  Bale command handler. Kept in their own module to avoid
  circular imports.
*/

export type StatusSnapshot = {
  connected: boolean;
  phase: string;
  loggingIn: boolean;
  needsLogin: boolean;
  paused: boolean;
  connectionBlocked: boolean;
  ownerId: string;
  sendTo: string[];
  watch: string[];
  autoAll: boolean;
  receiveText: boolean;
  receiveFiles: boolean;
  clock: string;
  clockInterval: number;
  floodUntil: number;
  droppedEvents: number;
  queued: number;
  baleOffset: number;
  uptimeSeconds: number;
  maxFileMB: number;
};

export function statusSnapshot(): StatusSnapshot {
  const s = state();
  const settings = s.settings;

  const connected =
    Boolean((s.client as { connected?: boolean } | null)?.connected) &&
    !s.login &&
    !s.runtime.needsLogin &&
    !s.runtime.paused;

  return {
    connected,
    phase: s.runtime.paused
      ? "paused"
      : connected
        ? "connected"
        : s.connectionPhase,
    loggingIn: Boolean(s.login),
    needsLogin: s.runtime.needsLogin,
    paused: s.runtime.paused,
    connectionBlocked: s.connectionBlocked,
    ownerId: settings.ownerId,
    sendTo: [...settings.sendTo],
    watch: [...settings.watch],
    autoAll: settings.autoAll,
    receiveText: settings.receiveText,
    receiveFiles: settings.receiveFiles,
    clock: settings.clock.mode,
    clockInterval: settings.clock.intervalSeconds,
    floodUntil: s.runtime.floodUntil,
    droppedEvents: s.droppedEvents,
    queued: s.queuedCount,
    baleOffset: s.runtime.baleOffset,
    uptimeSeconds: Math.floor((Date.now() - s.startedAt) / 1000),
    maxFileMB: Number(process.env.MAX_FILE_MB) || 20,
  };
}

export async function pauseBridge(): Promise<void> {
  const s = state();
  s.runtime.paused = true;
  saveRuntime();

  await closeOldConnection();
  s.connectionPhase = "paused";
  log("info", "Bridge paused; Telegram connection closed.");
}

export async function resumeBridge(): Promise<void> {
  const s = state();
  s.runtime.paused = false;
  saveRuntime();

  await connectSaved();
  log("info", "Bridge resumed.");
}

/*
  Restores the session from the pinned channel backup and reconnects.
  The channel copy always wins: it is downloaded again, decrypted in
  memory and used for the fresh connection.
*/
export async function restoreNow(): Promise<StatusSnapshot> {
  const s = state();

  if (s.connectionBlocked) {
    throw new Error("Restart this process before restoring.");
  }
  if (s.login) {
    throw new Error("Cancel the pending login first.");
  }
  if (s.runtime.needsLogin) {
    throw new Error(
      "This session was rejected. A fresh login is required, not restoration."
    );
  }

  await closeOldConnection();

  const session = await readChannelBackup();
  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("No pinned encrypted session was found.");
  }

  await connectSaved();
  return statusSnapshot();
}

/* Uploads the CURRENT live session to the channel and pins it. */
export async function backupNowSafe(): Promise<void> {
  await telegram(async (client) => {
    await uploadBackup(client.session.save() as unknown as string);
    return true;
  });
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/bale.ts
##############################################################################
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { getSettings, saveSettings, getRuntime, saveRuntime } from "./settings";
import { log } from "./store";
import { baleGetUpdates, bot, tell, botDownload, withRetry } from "./botapi";
import {
  reconnectNow,
  sendText,
  sendFile,
  getDialogs,
  formatResults,
  fetchChannelFile,
  pressCallback,
  recordError,
} from "./telegram";
import { backupNowSafe, pauseBridge, resumeBridge, statusSnapshot } from "./actions";
import { MAX_BYTES } from "./env";
import { state } from "./state";

/*
  Bale side: long-polling loop, owner pairing, slash commands and
  forwarding of normal messages/files to the configured targets.
  Exactly ONE getUpdates consumer may exist for the bot token.
*/

type BaleMedia = {
  file_id: string;
  file_size?: number;
  file_name?: string;
  mime_type?: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
};

type BaleMessage = {
  message_id: number;
  from?: { id: number };
  chat: { id: number; type: string };
  text?: string;
  caption?: string;
  photo?: BaleMedia[];
  document?: BaleMedia;
  video?: BaleMedia;
  audio?: BaleMedia;
  voice?: BaleMedia;
  animation?: BaleMedia;
  video_note?: BaleMedia;
  sticker?: BaleMedia;
};

type BaleCallback = {
  id: string;
  from?: { id: number };
  data?: string;
};

type BaleUpdate = {
  update_id: number;
  message?: BaleMessage;
  callback_query?: BaleCallback;
};

/* ---------- owner pairing ---------- */

function toAsciiDigits(value: string): string {
  const fa = "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹";
  const ar = "٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩";
  return String(value || "").replace(/[۰-۹٠-٩]/g, (digit) => {
    const faIndex = fa.indexOf(digit);
    return String(faIndex >= 0 ? faIndex : ar.indexOf(digit));
  });
}

export function createPairing(
  open = false
): { command: string; expiresAt: number; open: boolean } {
  const s = state();

  if (s.settings.ownerId) {
    throw new Error("A Bale owner is already paired.");
  }

  const code = String(crypto.randomInt(0, 1000000)).padStart(6, "0");

  s.pairing = {
    code,
    expiresAt: Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000,
    open: Boolean(open),
  };

  return {
    command: open ? "" : "/pair " + code,
    expiresAt: s.pairing.expiresAt,
    open: Boolean(open),
  };
}

export function unpairOwner(): { ok: true } {
  if (process.env.BALE_OWNER_ID) {
    throw new Error("Remove BALE_OWNER_ID from .env before unpairing.");
  }

  const s = state();
  s.pairing = null;
  s.settings.ownerId = "";
  saveSettings();

  return { ok: true };
}

/* ---------- settings updates coming from owner commands ---------- */

function splitList(value: unknown): string[] {
  const array = Array.isArray(value)
    ? value
    : String(value || "").split(/[,\n]/);
  return [...new Set(array.map((x) => String(x).trim()).filter(Boolean))];
}

export async function updateWatch(value: unknown): Promise<void> {
  const items = splitList(value);
  if (items.length > 100) throw new Error("Too many watched targets.");
  getSettings().watch = items;
  saveSettings();
}

export async function updateSendTo(value: unknown): Promise<void> {
  const items = splitList(value);
  if (items.length > 10) throw new Error("Maximum 10 send destinations.");
  getSettings().sendTo = items;
  saveSettings();
}

/* ---------- owner commands ---------- */

const HELP = [
  "دستورات پل بله و تلگرام:",
  "",
  "/id",
  "/status",
  "/dialogs",
  "/to @channel1,-1001234567890",
  "/send @channel1 متن یا HTML",
  "/receive x-0 .mp4 @spartan_yt",
  "/receive x-2 .pdf -1001234567890",
  "/btn 1",
  "/auto on | /auto off",
  "/watch -1001234567890,123456789",
  "/watch *",
  "/watch off",
  "/clock off | name | bio | both",
  "/backup",
  "/reconnect",
  "/pause",
  "/resume",
  "",
  "• /receive: دریافت آخرین فایل با پسوند داده‌شده از هر گروه یا کانال (۲۰۰ پیام آخر بررسی می‌شود)؛ x-0 آخرین فایل، x-1 یکی قبل از آن و ...؛ پسوند * یعنی هر رسانه‌ای؛ سقف حجم ۵۰ مگابایت.",
  "• هر پیام خصوصی تلگرام (متن، فایل، فیلم و ...) خودکار به همین‌جا می‌رسد؛ /auto off خاموش می‌کند.",
  "• دکمه‌های شیشه‌ای ربات‌های تلگرامی زیر پیام‌شان در بله ظاهر می‌شوند و قابل کلیک هستند (/btn شماره هم کار می‌کند).",
  "• پس از /to، متن و فایل عادی به مقصدهای ذخیره‌شده ارسال می‌شوند.",
  "• فایل‌ها برای حفظ فرمت به‌صورت Document منتقل می‌شوند.",
].join("\n");

type Command = { name: string; args: string };

function parseCommand(text: string): Command | null {
  const match = String(text).match(
    /^\/([a-z]+)(?:@[a-z0-9_]+)?(?:\s+([\s\S]*))?$/
  );
  if (!match) return null;
  return { name: match[1].toLowerCase(), args: (match[2] || "").trim() };
}

async function replyTo(chatId: string, text: string): Promise<void> {
  await bot("bale", "sendMessage", { chat_id: chatId, text });
}

function pairingHint(ownerPaired: boolean): string {
  if (ownerPaired) {
    return (
      "این ربات، کنترل‌گر خصوصی پل بله ← تلگرام است و فقط مالک متصل " +
      "می‌تواند فرمان بدهد."
    );
  }
  return (
    "این ربات هنوز مالکی ندارد؛ کافی است هر پیامی بفرستید تا حساب شما " +
    "بلافاصله مالک ربات شود."
  );
}

async function handleBale(message: BaleMessage): Promise<void> {
  if (message?.chat?.type !== "private") return;

  const s = state();
  const userId = String(message.from?.id || "");
  const chatId = String(message.chat?.id || "");
  const text = String(message.text || "").trim();
  const command = parseCommand(text);

  // /id always answers — it only reveals the requester's own ID.
  if (command?.name === "id") {
    await replyTo(chatId, "شناسه شما: " + userId);
    return;
  }

  const settings = s.settings;

  /*
    Auto-pair: while no owner is connected, the very first private
    account that messages the bot becomes the owner — no panel step,
    no code, no time window. The bot username stays private, so in
    this sandbox the first sender is the legitimate controller.
  */
  if (!settings.ownerId && userId && userId === chatId) {
    settings.ownerId = userId;
    s.pairing = null;
    saveSettings();
    log("info", "Bale owner auto-paired: " + userId);
    await tell(
      "✅ حساب شما به‌عنوان مالک ربات ثبت شد و همه دستورها فعال شدند." +
        "\n\n" +
        HELP
    );
    return;
  }

  // Code pairing: the first private chat presenting a valid code wins.
  if (command?.name === "pair") {
    const pairing = s.pairing;

    if (settings.ownerId) {
      await replyTo(chatId, "مالک قبلاً متصل شده است.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      !pairing ||
      pairing.open ||
      pairing.expiresAt < Date.now() ||
      !userId ||
      userId !== chatId
    ) {
      await replyTo(chatId, pairingHint(false));
      return;
    }

    const supplied = Buffer.from(toAsciiDigits(command.args));
    const expected = Buffer.from(pairing.code);

    if (
      supplied.length !== expected.length ||
      !crypto.timingSafeEqual(supplied, expected)
    ) {
      await replyTo(chatId, "کد نادرست است. از پنل کد تازه بگیرید.");
      return;
    }

    s.pairing = null;
    settings.ownerId = userId;
    saveSettings();
    log("info", "Bale owner paired by code: " + userId);

    await tell("مالک بله متصل شد.\n\n" + HELP);
    return;
  }

  const paired =
    Boolean(settings.ownerId) &&
    userId === settings.ownerId &&
    chatId === settings.ownerId;

  if (!paired) {
    // Never stay silent — greet the sender and explain the next step.
    if (command?.name === "start" || command?.name === "help") {
      await replyTo(
        chatId,
        "سلام! 👋\n" +
          "این ربات، حساب تلگرام شما را از داخل بله کنترل می‌کند.\n\n" +
          "شناسه شما: " +
          userId +
          "\n\n" +
          pairingHint(Boolean(settings.ownerId))
      );
      return;
    }

    await replyTo(
      chatId,
      pairingHint(Boolean(settings.ownerId)) +
        "\n\nشناسه شما: " +
        userId
    );
    return;
  }

  if (command) {
    switch (command.name) {
      case "start":
      case "help":
        await tell(HELP);
        return;

      case "status":
        await tell(JSON.stringify(statusSnapshot(), null, 2));
        return;

      case "dialogs": {
        const dialogs = await getDialogs();
        await tell(
          dialogs
            .map(
              (item) =>
                item.id + " | " + item.title +
                (item.username ? " | @" + item.username : "")
            )
            .join("\n") || "گفتگویی یافت نشد."
        );
        return;
      }

      case "to":
        await updateSendTo(command.args);
        await tell("مقصدهای ارسال ذخیره شدند.");
        return;

      case "watch":
        await updateWatch(command.args === "off" ? [] : command.args);
        await tell("فهرست دریافت ذخیره شد.");
        return;

      case "receive": {
        const parts = command.args.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        let index = 0;
        let ext = "";
        const rest: string[] = [];

        for (const part of parts) {
          const indexMatch = part.match(/^x-?(\d+)$/i);
          if (indexMatch && !ext && rest.length === 0) {
            index = Number(indexMatch[1]);
            continue;
          }
          if (
            !ext &&
            (/^\.\w{1,12}$/.test(part) || part === "*" || part === ".*")
          ) {
            ext = part;
            continue;
          }
          rest.push(part);
        }

        const target = rest[0] || "";
        if (!target) {
          throw new Error("استفاده: /receive x-0 .mp4 @spartan_yt");
        }
        if (index > 99) {
          throw new Error("شماره فایل باید بین x-0 تا x-99 باشد.");
        }

        await tell("🔎 در حال جستجو و دریافت فایل از " + target + " ...");

        const found = await fetchChannelFile(target, ext, index);

        await withRetry(
          () =>
            bot(
              "bale",
              "sendDocument",
              {
                chat_id: settings.ownerId,
                caption: Array.from(
                  "📥 دریافت از: " + found.source +
                    " | پیام: " + found.msgId +
                    "\nنام: " + found.name +
                    " | حجم: " + (found.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2) + " MB" +
                    (found.joined
                      ? "\n🔒 برای دسترسی به تاریخچه، حساب به این کانال پیوست."
                      : "")
                ).slice(0, 900).join(""),
              },
              { buffer: found.buffer, name: found.name, mime: found.mime }
            ),
          2
        );
        return;
      }

      case "btn": {
        const number = Number(toAsciiDigits(command.args).trim());
        const list = s.lastButtonList;

        if (!list.length) {
          throw new Error("فعلاً دکمه‌ای برای فشردن ثبت نشده است.");
        }
        if (!number || number < 1 || number > list.length) {
          throw new Error("شماره دکمه باید بین ۱ تا " + list.length + " باشد.");
        }

        const entry = s.callbackMap.get(list[number - 1]);
        if (!entry) {
          throw new Error("این دکمه منقضی شده است؛ ربات دوباره پیام بفرستد.");
        }

        await tell("🔘 در حال فشردن دکمه " + number + " ...");
        await tell(await pressCallback(entry.chat, entry.msgId, entry.data));
        return;
      }

      case "auto": {
        const value = command.args.toLowerCase();
        if (value !== "on" && value !== "off") {
          throw new Error("استفاده: /auto on یا /auto off");
        }
        settings.autoAll = value === "on";
        saveSettings();
        await tell(
          "دریافت خودکار همه پیام‌های خصوصی تلگرام: " +
            (settings.autoAll ? "روشن ✅" : "خاموش ❌")
        );
        return;
      }

      case "send": {
        const separator = command.args.search(/\s/);
        if (separator < 1) {
          throw new Error("Use /send target1,target2 message");
        }
        const targets = command.args.slice(0, separator);
        const body = command.args.slice(separator).trim();
        await tell(formatResults(await sendText(targets, body)));
        return;
      }

      case "clock": {
        if (!["off", "name", "bio", "both"].includes(command.args)) {
          throw new Error("Use off, name, bio or both.");
        }
        settings.clock.mode = command.args as "off" | "name" | "bio" | "both";
        saveSettings();
        await tell("تنظیم ساعت ذخیره شد.");
        return;
      }

      case "backup":
        await backupNowSafe();
        await tell("سشن رمزگذاری، ارسال و سنجاق شد.");
        return;

      case "reconnect":
        await reconnectNow();
        await tell("وضعیت اتصال: " + statusSnapshot().connected);
        return;

      case "pause":
        await pauseBridge();
        await tell("پل متوقف شد. برای ادامه /resume");
        return;

      case "resume":
        await resumeBridge();
        await tell("وضعیت اتصال: " + statusSnapshot().connected);
        return;

      default:
        await tell("دستور ناشناخته است. /help");
        return;
    }
  }

  if (text.startsWith("/")) {
    await tell("دستور ناشناخته است. /help");
    return;
  }

  // Plain message or media from the owner -> forward to sendTo targets.
  const photos = Array.isArray(message.photo) ? message.photo : [];
  const photo = photos.length
    ? [...photos].sort(
        (a, b) =>
          Number(a.file_size || (a.width || 0) * (a.height || 0) || 0) -
          Number(b.file_size || (b.width || 0) * (b.height || 0) || 0)
      )[photos.length - 1]
    : null;

  const media =
    message.document ||
    message.video ||
    message.audio ||
    message.voice ||
    message.animation ||
    message.video_note ||
    message.sticker ||
    photo;

  if (media) {
    if (!settings.sendTo.length) {
      throw new Error("Select destinations with /to first.");
    }
    if (Number(media.file_size || 0) > MAX_BYTES) {
      throw new Error("File exceeds the transfer limit.");
    }

    const extensions: Record<string, string> = {
      "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
      "image/png": ".png",
      "image/webp": ".webp",
      "image/gif": ".gif",
      "video/mp4": ".mp4",
      "video/webm": ".webm",
      "audio/mpeg": ".mp3",
      "audio/ogg": ".ogg",
      "audio/mp4": ".m4a",
      "application/pdf": ".pdf",
      "application/zip": ".zip",
      "application/x-tgsticker": ".tgs",
    };

    let fallback = ".bin";
    if (media === photo) fallback = ".jpg";
    if (media === message.voice) fallback = ".ogg";
    if (media === message.video || media === message.video_note) {
      fallback = ".mp4";
    }

    const name =
      media.file_name ||
      "bale-" + message.message_id +
        (extensions[media.mime_type || ""] || fallback);

    const bytes = await botDownload("bale", media.file_id);

    await tell(
      formatResults(
        await sendFile(settings.sendTo, bytes, name, message.caption || "")
      )
    );
    return;
  }

  if (text) {
    await tell(formatResults(await sendText(settings.sendTo, text)));
    return;
  }

  await tell("این نوع پیام پشتیبانی نمی‌شود.");
}

/* ---------- inline-button press (Bale callback queries) ---------- */

async function answerCallback(id: string, text: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await bot("bale", "answerCallbackQuery", {
      callback_query_id: id,
      text,
    });
  } catch {
    /* best-effort only */
  }
}

async function handleCallback(query: BaleCallback): Promise<void> {
  const ownerId = getSettings().ownerId;

  if (!ownerId || String(query.from?.id || "") !== ownerId) {
    await answerCallback(query.id, "فقط مالک ربات.");
    return;
  }

  const key = String(query.data || "").match(/^p:([0-9a-f]+)$/)?.[1];
  if (!key) {
    await answerCallback(query.id, "دکمه ناشناخته.");
    return;
  }

  const entry = state().callbackMap.get(key);
  if (!entry) {
    await answerCallback(query.id, "این دکمه منقضی شده است.");
    return;
  }

  await answerCallback(query.id, "در حال ارسال کلیک به تلگرام ...");

  try {
    await tell(
      "🔘 " +
        (await pressCallback(entry.chat, entry.msgId, entry.data))
    );
  } catch (error) {
    await tell("خطا در فشردن دکمه: " + recordError(error));
  }
}

/* ---------- long-poll loop ---------- */

export function startBalePolling(): void {
  const s = state();
  if (s.polling) return;
  s.polling = true;
  void pollBale();
}

export function stopBalePolling(): void {
  state().polling = false;
}

async function pollBale(): Promise<void> {
  log("info", "Bale polling started.");

  for (;;) {
    if (!state().polling) return;

    try {
      const runtime = getRuntime();
      const updates = (await baleGetUpdates(
        runtime.baleOffset
      )) as BaleUpdate[];

      for (const update of updates || []) {
        const updateId = Number(update.update_id);

        // Exactly-once guard: skip any update this process has
        // already handled (re-delivery, offset regression, ...).
        const seen = state().processedUpdates;
        if (seen.has(updateId)) continue;
        seen.add(updateId);
        if (seen.size > 600) {
          let trim = 100;
          for (const value of seen) {
            if (trim-- <= 0) break;
            seen.delete(value);
          }
        }

        // Advance the offset BEFORE side effects: after a crash a
        // command may be skipped, never duplicated.
        runtime.baleOffset = updateId + 1;
        saveRuntime();

        if (update.callback_query) {
          try {
            await handleCallback(update.callback_query);
          } catch (error) {
            log(
              "error",
              "Bale callback failed: " + (error as Error)?.message
            );
          }
          continue;
        }

        if (!update.message) continue;
        try {
          await handleBale(update.message);
        } catch (error) {
          const message = (error as Error)?.message || String(error);
          log("error", "Bale command failed: " + message);
          if (String(update.message.from?.id) === getSettings().ownerId) {
            await tell("خطا: " + message).catch(() => {});
          }
        }
      }

      if (!updates?.length) {
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      log("error", "Bale polling: " + (error as Error)?.message);
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000));
    }
  }
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/botapi.ts
##############################################################################
import { CONFIG, MAX_BYTES } from "./env";
import { log } from "./store";
import { getSettings } from "./settings";

/*
  Unified Bot API client for BOTH sides:
  - "bale"     -> tapi.bale.ai      (controller bot, talks to the owner)
  - "telegram" -> api.telegram.org  (backup bot, session file transport)
*/

export type BotKind = "bale" | "telegram";

export type BinaryAttachment = {
  buffer: Buffer;
  name: string;
  mime?: string;
  field?: string;
};

function botBase(kind: BotKind): string {
  return kind === "bale"
    ? "https://tapi.bale.ai/bot" + CONFIG.BALE_BOT_TOKEN
    : "https://api.telegram.org/bot" + CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_BOT_TOKEN;
}

export async function readLimited(
  response: Response,
  limit: number
): Promise<Buffer> {
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error("Remote HTTP status: " + response.status);
  }

  const declared = Number(response.headers.get("content-length") || 0);
  if (declared > limit) {
    throw new Error("Remote content exceeds the size limit.");
  }

  if (!response.body) {
    throw new Error("Remote response has no body.");
  }

  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  try {
    for (;;) {
      const part = await reader.read();
      if (part.done) break;

      total += part.value.length;
      if (total > limit) {
        throw new Error("Remote content exceeds the size limit.");
      }

      chunks.push(Buffer.from(part.value));
    }
  } finally {
    await reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
  }

  return Buffer.concat(chunks);
}

export async function bot<T = unknown>(
  kind: BotKind,
  method: string,
  data: Record<string, unknown> = {},
  binary: BinaryAttachment | null = null
): Promise<T> {
  let body: BodyInit;
  let headers: Record<string, string> | undefined;

  if (binary) {
    const form = new FormData();
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
      form.append(
        key,
        typeof value === "object" ? JSON.stringify(value) : String(value)
      );
    }
    form.append(
      binary.field || "document",
      new Blob([binary.buffer], {
        type: binary.mime || "application/octet-stream",
      }),
      binary.name
    );
    body = form;
  } else {
    headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
    body = JSON.stringify(data);
  }

  const response = await fetch(botBase(kind) + "/" + method, {
    method: "POST",
    headers,
    body,
    redirect: "error",
    // Large binary uploads/downloads (up to 50 MB) need a long window.
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(binary ? 300000 : 40000),
  });

  const bytes = await readLimited(response, 8 * 1024 * 1024);
  const out = JSON.parse(bytes.toString("utf8"));

  if (!out.ok) throw new Error(kind + ": " + out.description);
  return out.result as T;
}

export async function botDownload(
  kind: BotKind,
  fileId: string,
  limit: number = MAX_BYTES
): Promise<Buffer> {
  const file = await bot<{ file_size?: number; file_path?: string }>(
    kind,
    "getFile",
    { file_id: fileId }
  );

  if (Number(file.file_size || 0) > limit) {
    throw new Error("File exceeds the transfer limit.");
  }

  const relative = String(file.file_path || "");
  if (!relative || relative.includes("..") || relative.includes("://")) {
    throw new Error("Invalid bot file path.");
  }

  const base =
    kind === "bale"
      ? "https://tapi.bale.ai/file/bot" + CONFIG.BALE_BOT_TOKEN + "/"
      : "https://api.telegram.org/file/bot" + CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_BOT_TOKEN + "/";

  const response = await fetch(base + relative.replace(/^\/+/, ""), {
    redirect: "error",
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(300000),
  });

  return readLimited(response, limit);
}

/*
  Bale/Telegram endpoints occasionally drop a request ("fetch failed",
  transient 5xx). Retry flaky calls instead of failing the command.
*/
export async function withRetry<T>(
  fn: () => Promise<T>,
  attempts = 2,
  delayMs = 2000
): Promise<T> {
  let lastError: unknown;

  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      if (attempt < attempts) {
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs));
      }
    }
  }

  throw lastError;
}

/* Notify the Bale owner; long texts are split into 3500-char chunks. */
export async function tell(text: string): Promise<void> {
  const owner = getSettings().ownerId;
  if (!owner) return;

  const chars = Array.from(String(text));
  for (let i = 0; i < chars.length; i += 3500) {
    await bot("bale", "sendMessage", {
      chat_id: owner,
      text: chars.slice(i, i + 3500).join(""),
    });
  }
}

export function safeFilename(value: unknown): string {
  return (
    String(value || "file.bin")
      .replace(/[\/\\\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, "_")
      .slice(0, 180) || "file.bin"
  );
}

export async function baleGetUpdates(offset: number): Promise<unknown[]> {
  return bot<unknown[]>("bale", "getUpdates", {
    offset,
    timeout: 25,
    limit: 30,
    allowed_updates: ["message", "callback_query"],
  });
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/clock.ts
##############################################################################
import { Api } from "telegram";
import { exclusive, recordError } from "./telegram";
import { saveSettings, saveRuntime, getRuntime } from "./settings";
import { state } from "./state";

/*
  Iran-time profile clock: periodically appends the Tehran clock to
  the account's first name and/or bio. The original values are
  captured once so they can always be restored by mode "off".
*/

function truncate(value: string, maximum: number): string {
  return Array.from(String(value || "")).slice(0, maximum).join("");
}

export async function tickClock(): Promise<void> {
  const s = state();
  const mode = s.settings.clock.mode;

  if (mode === "off" && !s.runtime.clockApplied) return;

  const bucket =
    mode + ":" +
    Math.floor(Date.now() / (s.settings.clock.intervalSeconds * 1000));

  if (bucket === s.clockBucket) return;

  await exclusive(async () => {
    const client = state().client as unknown as {
      connected?: boolean;
      getMe: () => Promise<unknown>;
      invoke: (params: unknown) => Promise<unknown>;
    } | null;
    if (!client?.connected) return;

    if (!s.settings.clock.captured) {
      const me = (await client.getMe()) as unknown as {
        firstName?: string;
      };
      const full = await client.invoke(
        new Api.users.GetFullUser({ id: new Api.InputUserSelf() })
      );

      s.settings.clock.baseName = me.firstName || "User";
      s.settings.clock.baseBio =
        (full.fullUser as unknown as { about?: string }).about || "";
      s.settings.clock.captured = true;
      saveSettings();
    }

    const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
      timeZone: "Asia/Tehran",
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      hourCycle: "h23",
    }).format(new Date());

    const withTime = (base: string, limit: number) =>
      truncate(base, limit - 8) + " | " + time;

    const firstName =
      mode === "name" || mode === "both"
        ? withTime(s.settings.clock.baseName, 64)
        : s.settings.clock.baseName;

    const about =
      mode === "bio" || mode === "both"
        ? withTime(s.settings.clock.baseBio, 70)
        : s.settings.clock.baseBio;

    await client.invoke(
      new Api.account.UpdateProfile({ firstName, about })
    );

    getRuntime().clockApplied = mode !== "off";
    saveRuntime();
    state().clockBucket = bucket;
  }).catch((error) => {
    recordError(error);
  });
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/crypto.ts
##############################################################################
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { KEY } from "./env";

/*
  AES-256-GCM envelope used for local state files AND for the
  session backup uploaded to the private Telegram channel.
  Without this exact key the channel backup is unreadable.
*/

export function seal(value: unknown): Buffer {
  const iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", KEY, iv);

  const data = Buffer.concat([
    cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"),
    cipher.final(),
  ]);

  return Buffer.from(
    JSON.stringify({
      version: 1,
      iv: iv.toString("base64"),
      tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"),
      data: data.toString("base64"),
    })
  );
}

export function unseal(bytes: Buffer): unknown {
  const box = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(bytes).toString("utf8"));

  if (box.version !== 1) {
    throw new Error("Unsupported encrypted file version.");
  }

  const iv = Buffer.from(box.iv, "base64");
  const tag = Buffer.from(box.tag, "base64");

  if (iv.length !== 12 || tag.length !== 16) {
    throw new Error("Invalid encrypted envelope.");
  }

  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", KEY, iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);

  return JSON.parse(
    Buffer.concat([
      decipher.update(Buffer.from(box.data, "base64")),
      decipher.final(),
    ]).toString("utf8")
  );
}

export function randomToken(bytes = 18): string {
  return crypto.randomBytes(bytes).toString("hex");
}

export function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  const left = Buffer.from(a);
  const right = Buffer.from(b);
  if (left.length !== right.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(left, right);
}

export function hmac(value: string): string {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", KEY).update(value).digest("base64url");
}

export function sha256(value: string): Buffer {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(value).digest();
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/env.ts
##############################################################################
/*
  Central configuration. All credentials come from fixed environment
  variables in .env — nothing is generated at runtime, so values
  survive restarts and channel backups stay readable.
*/

function required(name: string): string {
  const value = process.env[name];
  if (!value || /^(PUT_|REPLACE)/.test(value)) {
    throw new Error(
      "Set the environment variable " + name + " before starting."
    );
  }
  return value;
}

export const CONFIG = {
  TG_API_ID: Number(required("TG_API_ID")),
  TG_API_HASH: required("TG_API_HASH"),
  BALE_BOT_TOKEN: required("BALE_BOT_TOKEN"),
  TG_BACKUP_BOT_TOKEN: required("TG_BACKUP_BOT_TOKEN"),
  TG_BACKUP_CHANNEL_ID: required("TG_BACKUP_CHANNEL_ID"),
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "",
  SIMPLE_LOGIN: process.env.SIMPLE_LOGIN === "1",
  ENCRYPTION_KEY_BASE64: process.env.ENCRYPTION_KEY_BASE64 || "",
  DEFAULT_PHONE: (process.env.DEFAULT_PHONE || "").trim(),
  COOKIE_SECURE: process.env.COOKIE_SECURE === "1",
  /*
    50 MB is the practical Bot API upload limit (Bale and Telegram).
    Both auto-forward and /receive depend on this ceiling.
  */
  MAX_FILE_MB: Math.min(
    50,
    Math.max(1, Number(process.env.MAX_FILE_MB) || 50)
  ),
} as const;

if (!Number.isInteger(CONFIG.TG_API_ID) || CONFIG.TG_API_ID < 1) {
  throw new Error("TG_API_ID must be a positive integer.");
}

/*
  In SIMPLE_LOGIN mode the dashboard opens with one click and no
  password. Otherwise a fixed password of >= 16 chars is required.
*/
if (
  !CONFIG.SIMPLE_LOGIN &&
  (!CONFIG.ADMIN_PASSWORD || CONFIG.ADMIN_PASSWORD.length < 16)
) {
  throw new Error(
    "ADMIN_PASSWORD must contain at least 16 characters."
  );
}

export const KEY = Buffer.from(
  CONFIG.ENCRYPTION_KEY_BASE64,
  "base64"
);

if (KEY.length !== 32) {
  throw new Error(
    "ENCRYPTION_KEY_BASE64 must decode to exactly 32 bytes."
  );
}

export const MAX_BYTES = CONFIG.MAX_FILE_MB * 1024 * 1024;

### FILE: src/lib/bridge/index.ts
##############################################################################
import { state } from "./state";
import { log } from "./store";
import { tickClock } from "./clock";
import { closeOldConnection, connectSaved, recordError } from "./telegram";
import {
  acquireBridgeLock,
  releaseBridgeLock,
  touchBridgeLock,
} from "./lock";
import {
  statusSnapshot,
  pauseBridge,
  resumeBridge,
  restoreNow,
  backupNowSafe,
} from "./actions";
import {
  createPairing,
  unpairOwner,
  updateWatch,
  updateSendTo,
  startBalePolling,
} from "./bale";
import { getSettings, saveSettings, saveRuntime } from "./settings";

/*
  Orchestrator. `ensureBridge()` starts the polling loop and the
  maintenance interval exactly once per PROCESS — the guard flag
  lives on globalThis, so multiple module contexts (instrumentation
  + route bundles) can safely call it.
*/

export { statusSnapshot };
export { pauseBridge, resumeBridge, restoreNow, backupNowSafe };
export { createPairing, unpairOwner, updateWatch, updateSendTo };

/* Settings updates from the dashboard (validated). */
export async function updateSettings(patch: {
  sendTo?: unknown;
  watch?: unknown;
  autoAll?: unknown;
  receiveText?: unknown;
  receiveFiles?: unknown;
  clock?: { mode?: string; intervalSeconds?: unknown };
}): Promise<void> {
  const settings = getSettings();

  if (patch.sendTo !== undefined) await updateSendTo(patch.sendTo);
  if (patch.watch !== undefined) await updateWatch(patch.watch);

  if (patch.autoAll !== undefined) {
    settings.autoAll = Boolean(patch.autoAll);
  }

  if (patch.receiveText !== undefined) {
    settings.receiveText = Boolean(patch.receiveText);
  }
  if (patch.receiveFiles !== undefined) {
    settings.receiveFiles = Boolean(patch.receiveFiles);
  }

  if (patch.clock) {
    if (patch.clock.mode !== undefined) {
      if (!["off", "name", "bio", "both"].includes(patch.clock.mode)) {
        throw new Error("Invalid clock mode.");
      }
      settings.clock.mode = patch.clock.mode as
        | "off"
        | "name"
        | "bio"
        | "both";
    }
    if (patch.clock.intervalSeconds !== undefined) {
      settings.clock.intervalSeconds = Math.max(
        60,
        Number(patch.clock.intervalSeconds) || 300
      );
    }
  }

  saveSettings();
  log("info", "Settings updated from dashboard.");
}

export function clearRuntimeError(): void {
  state().droppedEvents = 0;
  saveRuntime();
  log("info", "Runtime counters cleared.");
}

/* ---------- maintenance loop ---------- */

async function maintenance(): Promise<void> {
  const s = state();

  try {
    // Expire stale login attempts after 10 minutes.
    if (
      s.login &&
      Date.now() - s.login.started > 10 * 60 * 1000
    ) {
      await closeOldConnection();
      s.login = null;
      log("info", "Stale login attempt expired.");
    }

    if (!s.runtime.paused && !s.login) {
      await connectSaved();
    }

    if (
      statusSnapshot().connected &&
      Date.now() >= s.runtime.floodUntil
    ) {
      await tickClock();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    recordError(error);
  }
}

export function ensureBridge(): void {
  const s = state();
  if (s.started) return;
  s.started = true;

  /*
    Only ONE process may run the bridge (polling + Telegram
    connection). In dev mode Next.js can spawn several worker
    processes; the lock file keeps exactly one poller alive.
  */
  if (!acquireBridgeLock()) {
    log("info", "Bridge lock held by another process — staying idle here.");
    return;
  }

  s.lockTimer = setInterval(touchBridgeLock, 20_000);

  log(
    "info",
    "Bridge starting — Telegram channel is the session source of truth."
  );

  startBalePolling();

  // First connection attempt after a short settle delay.
  setTimeout(() => {
    void maintenance();
  }, 1500);

  s.maintenanceTimer = setInterval(maintenance, 15000);

  const shutdown = () => {
    const current = state();
    if (current.maintenanceTimer) {
      clearInterval(current.maintenanceTimer);
      current.maintenanceTimer = null;
    }
    if (current.lockTimer) {
      clearInterval(current.lockTimer);
      current.lockTimer = null;
    }
    releaseBridgeLock();
  };

  process.once("SIGINT", shutdown);
  process.once("SIGTERM", shutdown);
}

/*
  Auto-start whenever this module is imported anywhere (API route
  bundle, instrumentation, ...). Idempotent per process.
*/
if (process.env.NEXT_RUNTIME !== "edge") {
  Promise.resolve().then(() => {
    try {
      ensureBridge();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[bridge] auto-start failed:", error);
    }
  });
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/lock.ts
##############################################################################
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

/*
  Single-instance lock for the bridge.

  Next.js dev mode can run instrumentation and API routes in
  DIFFERENT worker processes. Without this lock each process would
  start its own Bale long-poller and its own Telegram connection —
  producing duplicated replies and Telegram AUTH_KEY conflicts.

  The first process to create .data/bridge.lock exclusively wins and
  runs the bridge; every other process stays idle. The holder
  refreshes the lock mtime as a heartbeat; a lock that has not been
  touched for STALE_MS is considered abandoned (crashed process) and
  may be stolen.
*/

const LOCK_PATH = path.join(
  process.env.BRIDGE_DATA_DIR || path.join(process.cwd(), ".data"),
  "bridge.lock"
);
const STALE_MS = 60_000;

export function acquireBridgeLock(): boolean {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    try {
      const fd = fs.openSync(LOCK_PATH, "wx");
      fs.writeSync(fd, JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, at: Date.now() }));
      fs.closeSync(fd);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException)?.code !== "EEXIST") {
        return false;
      }
      try {
        const stat = fs.statSync(LOCK_PATH);
        if (Date.now() - stat.mtimeMs > STALE_MS) {
          fs.unlinkSync(LOCK_PATH);
          continue;
        }
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
      return false;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/* Heartbeat: keep the lock mtime fresh while this process is alive. */
export function touchBridgeLock(): void {
  const now = new Date();
  try {
    fs.utimesSync(LOCK_PATH, now, now);
  } catch {}
}

/* Only remove the lock if this process actually owns it. */
export function releaseBridgeLock(): void {
  try {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(LOCK_PATH, "utf8");
    if (JSON.parse(raw).pid === process.pid) {
      fs.unlinkSync(LOCK_PATH);
    }
  } catch {}
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/session.ts
##############################################################################
import { TelegramClient } from "telegram";
import { CONFIG } from "./env";
import { seal, unseal } from "./crypto";
import { log } from "./store";
import { bot, botDownload } from "./botapi";

/*
  The private Telegram channel is the ONLY source of truth for the
  MTProto session. There is no trusted local session file and no
  cached fallback: every fresh connection downloads the pinned
  encrypted backup again, decrypts it in memory and connects.
*/

type SessionBackup = {
  kind: string;
  version: number;
  createdAt: string;
  session: string;
};

function sessionBackupObject(session: string): SessionBackup {
  return {
    kind: "telegram-mtproto-session",
    version: 1,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    session,
  };
}

export async function privateBackupChannel(): Promise<{
  id: number;
  title: string;
}> {
  const chat = await bot<{
    id: number;
    title: string;
    type: string;
    username?: string;
  }>("telegram", "getChat", { chat_id: CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_CHANNEL_ID });

  if (chat.type !== "channel" || chat.username) {
    throw new Error(
      "The backup destination must be a PRIVATE Telegram channel."
    );
  }

  return chat;
}

/* Encrypt, upload and pin the session file in the private channel. */
export async function uploadBackup(session: string): Promise<void> {
  await privateBackupChannel();

  const bytes = seal(sessionBackupObject(session));

  const posted = await bot<{ message_id: number }>(
    "telegram",
    "sendDocument",
    {
      chat_id: CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_CHANNEL_ID,
      caption:
        "Encrypted Telegram session backup\n" +
        "AES-256-GCM — the encryption key lives outside Telegram.",
    },
    { buffer: bytes, name: "telegram-session.enc" }
  );

  await bot("telegram", "pinChatMessage", {
    chat_id: CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_CHANNEL_ID,
    message_id: posted.message_id,
    disable_notification: true,
  });

  log("info", "Session file encrypted, uploaded and pinned.");
}

/* Download + decrypt the pinned session backup. null when absent. */
export async function readChannelBackup(): Promise<string | null> {
  const chat = await bot<{
    pinned_message?: {
      document?: { file_id: string; file_name?: string };
    };
  }>("telegram", "getChat", { chat_id: CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_CHANNEL_ID });

  const document = chat.pinned_message?.document;
  if (!document) return null;

  if (document.file_name !== "telegram-session.enc") {
    throw new Error(
      "The latest pinned message is not telegram-session.enc."
    );
  }

  const bytes = await botDownload("telegram", document.file_id, 256 * 1024);
  const backup = unseal(bytes) as Partial<SessionBackup>;

  if (
    backup.kind !== "telegram-mtproto-session" ||
    backup.version !== 1 ||
    typeof backup.session !== "string" ||
    !backup.session
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid encrypted session backup.");
  }

  return backup.session;
}

/* After login, push a fresh pinned backup of the live session. */
export async function backupSessionOf(client: TelegramClient): Promise<void> {
  const session = client.session.save() as unknown as string;
  await uploadBackup(session);
}

import { load, save } from "./store";
import { hydrateFromDisk, state } from "./state";

/*
  Settings + runtime accessors, all backed by the globalThis state
  so every module context sees identical values. Values are loaded
  from the encrypted .data files the first time this module is
  imported in the process.
*/

let hydrated = false;

function ensureHydrated(): void {
  if (hydrated) return;
  hydrated = true;
  hydrateFromDisk(load);
}

export function getSettings() {
  ensureHydrated();
  return state().settings;
}

export function getRuntime() {
  ensureHydrated();
  return state().runtime;
}

export function saveSettings(): void {
  save("settings", state().settings);
}

export function saveRuntime(): void {
  save("runtime", state().runtime);
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/state.ts
##############################################################################
import type { ConnectionPhase } from "./telegram-types";

/*
  The ONE mutable state of the bridge lives on globalThis.

  Next.js (Turbopack) can load the bridge modules in more than one
  module context (instrumentation graph AND route graphs). Module
  scoped variables would then be duplicated. By keeping every
  mutable value inside a single globalThis object, all contexts
  share the exact same runtime state: logs, settings, the GramJS
  client, queues and flags.
*/

export type LogRow = {
  time: string;
  level: "info" | "error";
  message: string;
};

export type ClockSettings = {
  mode: "off" | "name" | "bio" | "both";
  intervalSeconds: number;
  captured: boolean;
  baseName: string;
  baseBio: string;
};

export type Settings = {
  ownerId: string;
  sendTo: string[];
  watch: string[];
  /* Mirror every PRIVATE chat (people + bots) to the Bale owner. */
  autoAll: boolean;
  receiveText: boolean;
  receiveFiles: boolean;
  clock: ClockSettings;
};

/* Telegram bot inline-button press relay: short id -> target info. */
export type CallbackEntry = {
  chat: string;
  msgId: number;
  data: string;
};

export type Runtime = {
  paused: boolean;
  needsLogin: boolean;
  floodUntil: number;
  baleOffset: number;
  clockApplied: boolean;
};

export type LoginState = {
  phone: string;
  started: number;
  phoneCodeHash?: string;
};

export type BridgeState = {
  /* logs */
  logs: LogRow[];
  lastError: string;

  /* auth rate limiting */
  authWindow: number;
  authFailures: number;

  /* persistent-in-memory values */
  settings: Settings;
  runtime: Runtime;

  /* telegram client */
  client: unknown | null;
  login: LoginState | null;
  connectionBlocked: boolean;
  connectionPhase: ConnectionPhase;
  lastHealthCheck: number;
  droppedEvents: number;

  /* exclusive queue */
  tgTail: Promise<unknown>;
  queuedCount: number;

  /* bale */
  pairing: { code: string; expiresAt: number; open?: boolean } | null;
  polling: boolean;
  processedUpdates: Set<number>;

  /* telegram inline-button relay */
  callbackMap: Map<string, CallbackEntry>;
  lastButtonList: string[];

  /* clock */
  clockBucket: string;

  /* lifecycle */
  started: boolean;
  lockTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  maintenanceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  startedAt: number;
};

const GLOBAL_KEY = "__baleTelegramBridgeState__";

function defaults(): BridgeState {
  const storedSettings = {
    ownerId: process.env.BALE_OWNER_ID || "",
    sendTo: [] as string[],
    watch: [] as string[],
    autoAll: true,
    receiveText: true,
    receiveFiles: true,
    clock: {
      mode: "off" as const,
      intervalSeconds: 300,
      captured: false,
      baseName: "",
      baseBio: "",
    },
  };

  return {
    logs: [],
    lastError: "",
    authWindow: Date.now(),
    authFailures: 0,
    settings: storedSettings,
    runtime: {
      paused: false,
      needsLogin: false,
      floodUntil: 0,
      baleOffset: 0,
      clockApplied: false,
    },
    client: null,
    login: null,
    connectionBlocked: false,
    connectionPhase: "offline",
    lastHealthCheck: 0,
    droppedEvents: 0,
    tgTail: Promise.resolve(),
    queuedCount: 0,
    pairing: null,
    polling: false,
    processedUpdates: new Set<number>(),
    callbackMap: new Map<string, CallbackEntry>(),
    lastButtonList: [],
    clockBucket: "",
    started: false,
    lockTimer: null,
    maintenanceTimer: null,
    startedAt: Date.now(),
  };
}

export function state(): BridgeState {
  const g = globalThis as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!g[GLOBAL_KEY]) {
    g[GLOBAL_KEY] = defaults();
  }
  return g[GLOBAL_KEY] as BridgeState;
}

/* Hydrate settings/runtime from the encrypted .data files once. */
export function hydrateFromDisk(
  loadFn: <T>(name: string, fallback: T) => T
): void {
  const s = state();

  const stored = loadFn<Partial<Settings>>("settings", {});
  s.settings = {
    ...s.settings,
    ...stored,
    clock: { ...s.settings.clock, ...(stored.clock || {}) },
  };

  const storedRuntime = loadFn<Partial<Runtime>>("runtime", {});
  s.runtime = { ...s.runtime, ...storedRuntime };
}

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/store.ts
##############################################################################
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { seal, unseal } from "./crypto";
import { CONFIG } from "./env";
import { state } from "./state";

/*
  Encrypted local storage (.data/*.enc) for settings and runtime
  counters. The Telegram session itself is NEVER stored here — the
  private Telegram channel is its only source of truth.
*/

/*
  Data directory: BRIDGE_DATA_DIR wins (set by the sandbox launcher so
  the standalone server — which chdirs into .next/standalone — keeps
  using the project's .data). Fallback: cwd/.data (deployed runtime).
*/
const DATA_DIR =
  process.env.BRIDGE_DATA_DIR || path.join(process.cwd(), ".data");

fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });

function storagePath(name: string): string {
  if (!/^[a-z0-9_-]+$/i.test(name)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid storage name.");
  }
  return path.join(DATA_DIR, name + ".enc");
}

export function save(name: string, value: unknown): void {
  const file = storagePath(name);
  const temporary = file + ".tmp";
  fs.writeFileSync(temporary, seal(value), { mode: 0o600 });
  fs.renameSync(temporary, file);
}

export function load<T>(name: string, fallback: T): T {
  const file = storagePath(name);
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return fallback;
  // Fails closed on corruption or a wrong key.
  return unseal(fs.readFileSync(file)) as T;
}

export function remove(name: string): void {
  try {
    fs.unlinkSync(storagePath(name));
  } catch {}
}

/* ---------- logging (backed by the global state) ---------- */

function redact(value: unknown): string {
  let text = String(value ?? "Unknown error");

  for (const secret of [
    CONFIG.TG_API_HASH,
    CONFIG.BALE_BOT_TOKEN,
    CONFIG.TG_BACKUP_BOT_TOKEN,
    CONFIG.ADMIN_PASSWORD,
    CONFIG.ENCRYPTION_KEY_BASE64,
  ]) {
    if (secret) text = text.split(secret).join("[REDACTED]");
  }

  return text.slice(0, 700);
}

export function log(level: "info" | "error", value: unknown): void {
  const s = state();
  const row = {
    time: new Date().toISOString(),
    level,
    message: redact(value),
  };

  s.logs.push(row);
  if (s.logs.length > 120) s.logs.shift();

  if (level === "error") s.lastError = row.message;

  console.log(row.time, row.level, row.message);
}

export function getLogs() {
  return [...state().logs].reverse();
}

export function getLastError(): string {
  return state().lastError;
}

export function errorText(error: unknown): string {
  const anyError = error as { errorMessage?: string; message?: string };
  return redact(anyError?.errorMessage || anyError?.message || error);
}
