/*
  Shared connection-phase type — kept free of heavy imports so the
  state module can reference it without cycles.
*/

export type ConnectionPhase =
  | "offline"
  | "needs-login"
  | "connecting"
  | "connected"
  | "paused";

##############################################################################
### FILE: src/lib/bridge/telegram.ts
##############################################################################
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import bigInt from "big-integer";
import {
  TelegramClient,
  Api,
  utils,
  sessions,
  password,
  client as telegramClientModule,
} from "telegram";

const { StringSession } = sessions;
const { computeCheck } = password;
const { CustomFile } = telegramClientModule.uploads;

import { CONFIG, MAX_BYTES } from "./env";
import { log } from "./store";
import { getRuntime, getSettings, saveRuntime } from "./settings";
import { readChannelBackup, uploadBackup } from "./session";
import { bot, safeFilename, tell, withRetry } from "./botapi";
import { state } from "./state";

/*
  MTProto side. The Telegram account is controlled through GramJS.
  Every operation is serialized through one exclusive queue, and
  fresh connections ALWAYS read the pinned channel backup again.
*/

export type { ConnectionPhase } from "./telegram-types";

type LoginState = {
  phone: string;
  started: number;
  phoneCodeHash?: string;
};

/* ---------- error classification ---------- */

export function recordError(error: unknown): string {
  const errorAny = error as { errorMessage?: string; message?: string };
  const message =
    errorAny?.errorMessage || errorAny?.message || String(error);

  const text = message.slice(0, 700);

  if (
    /AUTH_KEY_DUPLICATED|AUTH_KEY_UNREGISTERED|SESSION_REVOKED|SESSION_EXPIRED/.test(
      text
    )
  ) {
    const s = state();
    s.runtime.needsLogin = true;
    saveRuntime();
  }

  if (/FLOOD|SLOWMODE_WAIT/.test(text)) {
    const seconds =
      Number((error as { seconds?: number })?.seconds) ||
      Number(text.match(/(?:FLOOD_WAIT|SLOWMODE_WAIT)_(\d+)/)?.[1]) ||
      60;

    const s = state();
    s.runtime.floodUntil = Math.max(
      s.runtime.floodUntil,
      Date.now() + seconds * 1000
    );
    saveRuntime();
  }

  log("error", text);
  return text;
}

/* ---------- exclusive operation queue ---------- */

export function exclusive<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const s = state();

  if (s.queuedCount >= 100) {
    return Promise.reject(new Error("Telegram queue is full."));
  }

  s.queuedCount++;

  const result = s.tgTail.then(fn) as Promise<T>;
  s.tgTail = result
    .catch(() => {})
    .finally(() => {
      s.queuedCount--;
    });

  return result;
}

export function telegram<T>(
  fn: (client: TelegramClient) => Promise<T>
): Promise<T> {
  return exclusive(async () => {
    const s = state();
    const client = s.client as TelegramClient | null;

    if (s.connectionBlocked) {
      throw new Error(
        "Restart required: previous connection state uncertain."
      );
    }
    if (s.login) throw new Error("Telegram login is in progress.");
    if (!client || !client.connected) {
      throw new Error("Telegram is disconnected.");
    }
    if (Date.now() < s.runtime.floodUntil) {
      throw new Error("Telegram FloodWait cooldown is active.");
    }
    return fn(client);
  });
}

/* ---------- client lifecycle ---------- */

function makeClient(session: string): TelegramClient {
  const client = new TelegramClient(
    new StringSession(session),
    CONFIG.TG_API_ID,
    CONFIG.TG_API_HASH,
    {
      connectionRetries: 3,
      requestRetries: 2,
      retryDelay: 1000,
      autoReconnect: false,
      floodSleepThreshold: 0,
    }
  );

  client.setLogLevel(process.env.TG_DEBUG === "1" ? "debug" : "error");

  /*
    GramJS's NewMessage event builder proved unreliable inside this
    Next.js runtime: raw updates DO reach the dispatcher (verified
    via TG_DEBUG) but the builder layer silently dropped them.
    A raw observer is registered instead — it receives EVERY update
    and routes the message-bearing ones to the relay logic.
  */
  client.addEventHandler(onRawUpdate);

  return client;
}

export async function closeOldConnection(): Promise<void> {
  const s = state();
  const old = s.client as TelegramClient | null;
  s.client = null;

  if (old) {
    try {
      await old.destroy();
    } catch (error) {
      log("error", "Client destruction failed: " + (error as Error).message);
      s.connectionBlocked = true;
    }
  }
}

/*
  Fresh connection: the pinned channel backup is the single source.
  No local session file, no cached fallback (by design).
*/
export async function connectSaved(): Promise<void> {
  const s = state();
  const runtime = s.runtime;

  if (
    s.connectionBlocked ||
    runtime.paused ||
    runtime.needsLogin ||
    s.login ||
    ((s.client as TelegramClient | null)?.connected ?? false) ||
    Date.now() < runtime.floodUntil
  ) {
    return;
  }

  await closeOldConnection();

  let session: string | null = null;

  try {
    session = await readChannelBackup();
  } catch (error) {
    recordError(error);
    s.connectionPhase = "offline";
    return;
  }

  if (!session) {
    s.connectionPhase = "needs-login";
    log("info", "No pinned session backup — log in through the PWA.");
    return;
  }

  s.connectionPhase = "connecting";

  try {
    const client = makeClient(session);
    s.client = client;
    await client.connect();
    await client.invoke(new Api.updates.GetState());

    s.connectionPhase = "connected";
    s.lastHealthCheck = Date.now();
    s.connectionBlocked = false;

    log("info", "Encrypted session downloaded from Telegram and connected.");

    // Warm the peer cache so numeric IDs resolve instantly.
    try {
      await client.getDialogs({ limit: 100 });
    } catch (error) {
      recordError(error);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    recordError(error);
    await closeOldConnection();
    s.connectionPhase = "offline";
  }
}

export async function reconnectNow(): Promise<void> {
  await exclusive(async () => {
    const s = state();

    if (s.connectionBlocked) {
      throw new Error(
        "Restart required: connection destruction was uncertain."
      );
    }
    if (s.login) {
      throw new Error("Cancel the pending login first.");
    }
    if (s.runtime.paused) {
      throw new Error("Resume the bridge first.");
    }
    if (s.runtime.needsLogin) {
      throw new Error(
        "Session is invalid. A fresh login is required, not a reconnect."
      );
    }

    await closeOldConnection();
    await connectSaved();
  });
}

/* ---------- login flow (PWA wizard) ---------- */

async function finishLogin(): Promise<{
  ok: true;
  user: string;
  backupWarning: string;
}> {
  const s = state();
  const client = s.client as TelegramClient | null;
  if (!client) throw new Error("Login client disappeared.");

  await client.invoke(new Api.updates.GetState());
  const me = await client.getMe();

  let backupWarning = "";
  try {
    await uploadBackup(client.session.save() as unknown as string);
  } catch (error) {
    backupWarning = recordError(error);
  }

  s.login = null;
  s.connectionPhase = "connected";
  s.runtime.needsLogin = false;
  saveRuntime();

  log("info", "Telegram login finished; session backup uploaded.");

  return {
    ok: true as const,
    user: String((me as unknown as { id: bigInt.BigInteger }).id),
    backupWarning,
  };
}

export async function beginLogin(phone: string): Promise<{ next: string }> {
  return exclusive(async () => {
    const s = state();

    if (s.login) {
      throw new Error("A login is already in progress.");
    }

    await closeOldConnection();

    s.connectionPhase = "connecting";
    const client = makeClient("");
    s.client = client;
    s.login = { phone, started: Date.now() };

    try {
      await client.connect();
      const sent = await client.sendCode(
        { apiId: CONFIG.TG_API_ID, apiHash: CONFIG.TG_API_HASH },
        phone
      );
      s.login.phoneCodeHash = sent.phoneCodeHash;
      return { next: "code" };
    } catch (error) {
      await closeOldConnection();
      s.login = null;
      s.connectionPhase = "needs-login";
      recordError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  });
}

export async function submitCode(code: string): Promise<{
  next?: string;
  ok?: true;
  user?: string;
  backupWarning?: string;
}> {
  return exclusive(async () => {
    const s = state();
    const login = s.login;
    const client = s.client as TelegramClient | null;

    if (!login?.phoneCodeHash || !client) {
      throw new Error("Start the Telegram login first.");
    }

    try {
      await client.invoke(
        new Api.auth.SignIn({
          phoneNumber: login.phone,
          phoneCodeHash: login.phoneCodeHash,
          phoneCode: String(code || "").replace(/\s/g, ""),
        })
      );
    } catch (error) {
      const message = String(
        (error as { errorMessage?: string })?.errorMessage ||
          (error as Error)?.message
      );

      if (/SESSION_PASSWORD_NEEDED/.test(message)) {
        return { next: "password" };
      }
      throw error;
    }

    return finishLogin();
  });
}

export async function submitPassword(passwordValue: string): Promise<{
  ok?: true;
  user?: string;
  backupWarning?: string;
}> {
  return exclusive(async () => {
    const s = state();
    const client = s.client as TelegramClient | null;

    if (!s.login || !client) {
      throw new Error("Start the Telegram login first.");
    }

    const settings = await client.invoke(new Api.account.GetPassword());
    const check = await computeCheck(
      settings,
      String(passwordValue || "")
    );

    await client.invoke(new Api.auth.CheckPassword({ password: check }));

    return finishLogin();
  });
}

export async function cancelLogin(): Promise<void> {
  await exclusive(async () => {
    const s = state();
    if (s.login) await closeOldConnection();
    s.login = null;
    s.connectionPhase = s.runtime.paused ? "paused" : "needs-login";
  });
}

/* ---------- dialogs ---------- */

export type DialogRow = { id: string; title: string; username: string };

export async function getDialogs(): Promise<DialogRow[]> {
  return telegram(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.getDialogs({ limit: 100 });
    return result.map((dialog) => ({
      id: String(dialog.id),
      title: dialog.title || "",
      username:
        (dialog.entity as unknown as { username?: string })?.username || "",
    }));
  });
}

/* ---------- sending ---------- */

function splitList(value: unknown): string[] {
  const array = Array.isArray(value)
    ? value
    : String(value || "").split(/[,\n]/);
  return [
    ...new Set(array.map((x) => String(x).trim()).filter(Boolean)),
  ];
}

function parsePeer(value: string): bigInt.BigInteger | string {
  const text = String(value).trim();
  // Numeric strings must never be parsed as phone numbers.
  return /^-?\d+$/.test(text) ? bigInt(text) : text;
}

export function formatResults(rows: string[]): string {
  return rows.join("\n");
}

export async function sendText(
  targets: unknown,
  text: string
): Promise<string[]> {
  const list = splitList(targets);
  if (!list.length || list.length > 10) {
    throw new Error("Choose between 1 and 10 destinations.");
  }
  if (!String(text || "").trim() || Array.from(text).length > 4096) {
    throw new Error("Text must contain 1 to 4096 characters.");
  }

  const results: string[] = [];
  for (const target of list) {
    try {
      await telegram((client) =>
        client.sendMessage(parsePeer(target), {
          message: text,
          parseMode: "html",
          linkPreview: false,
        })
      );
      results.push(target + ": OK");
    } catch (error) {
      results.push(target + ": " + recordError(error));
    }
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
  }
  return results;
}

export async function sendFile(
  targets: unknown,
  buffer: Buffer,
  filename: string,
  caption: string
): Promise<string[]> {
  const list = splitList(targets);
  if (!list.length || list.length > 10) {
    throw new Error("Choose between 1 and 10 destinations.");
  }
  if (buffer.length > MAX_BYTES) {
    throw new Error("File is too large.");
  }

  const name = safeFilename(filename);
  const results: string[] = [];

  for (const target of list) {
    try {
      await telegram((client) =>
        client.sendFile(parsePeer(target), {
          file: new CustomFile(name, buffer.length, "", buffer),
          caption: Array.from(caption || "").slice(0, 900).join(""),
          parseMode: "html",
          forceDocument: true,
          workers: 1,
        })
      );
      results.push(target + ": OK");
    } catch (error) {
      results.push(target + ": " + recordError(error));
    }
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
  }
  return results;
}

export async function backupNow(): Promise<void> {
  await telegram(async (client) => {
    await uploadBackup(client.session.save() as unknown as string);
    return true;
  });
}

/* ---------- inline-button relay (Telegram bots -> Bale) ---------- */

export type RelayButton = {
  text: string;
  url?: string;
  /* base64 of the MTProto callback payload */
  data?: string;
};

export type RelayRow = RelayButton[];

/*
  Bot messages carry Api.ReplyInlineMarkup with rows of buttons.
  Only callback and URL buttons are relayed; every other kind
  (web-view, switch-inline, ...) is skipped silently.
*/
function extractRelayRows(markup: unknown): RelayRow[] {
  const rows = (markup as { rows?: { buttons?: unknown[] }[] })?.rows;
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return [];

  const out: RelayRow[] = [];

  for (const row of rows) {
    const buttons: RelayButton[] = [];

    for (const button of row.buttons || []) {
      const b = button as {
        className?: string;
        text?: string;
        url?: string;
        query?: Uint8Array;
      };

      if (b.className === "KeyboardButtonUrl" && b.url) {
        buttons.push({ text: String(b.text || "link"), url: b.url });
      } else if (b.className === "KeyboardButtonCallback" && b.query) {
        buttons.push({
          text: String(b.text || "button"),
          data: Buffer.from(b.query as Uint8Array).toString("base64"),
        });
      }
    }

    if (buttons.length) out.push(buttons);
  }

  return out;
}

/* Short id (fits in 64-byte callback_data) -> press target info. */
function registerCallback(
  dataB64: string,
  chat: string,
  msgId: number
): string {
  const s = state();

  for (const [key, value] of s.callbackMap) {
    if (
      value.data === dataB64 &&
      value.chat === chat &&
      value.msgId === msgId
    ) {
      return key;
    }
  }

  const id = crypto.randomBytes(4).toString("hex");
  s.callbackMap.set(id, { chat, msgId, data: dataB64 });

  if (s.callbackMap.size > 300) {
    const oldest = s.callbackMap.keys().next().value;
    if (oldest !== undefined) s.callbackMap.delete(oldest);
  }

  return id;
}

async function relayButtonsToBale(
  text: string,
  rows: RelayRow[],
  chat: string,
  msgId: number
): Promise<void> {
  const ownerId = getSettings().ownerId;

  const keyboard = rows.map((row) =>
    row.map((button) =>
      button.url
        ? { text: button.text, url: button.url }
        : {
            text: button.text,
            callback_data: "p:" + registerCallback(button.data!, chat, msgId),
          }
    )
  );

  await bot("bale", "sendMessage", {
    chat_id: ownerId,
    text,
    reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard },
  });

  const ids: string[] = [];
  for (const row of keyboard) {
    for (const button of row) {
      if (typeof button.callback_data === "string") {
        ids.push(button.callback_data.slice(2));
      }
    }
  }
  state().lastButtonList = ids;
}

/*
  Send text to the owner; attach inline buttons when present.
  If Bale rejects the keyboard, fall back to a numbered list the
  owner can press with /btn <n>.
*/
export async function relayWithButtons(
  text: string,
  rows: RelayRow[],
  chat: string,
  msgId: number
): Promise<void> {
  if (!rows.length) {
    await tell(text);
    return;
  }

  try {
    await relayButtonsToBale(text, rows, chat, msgId);
    return;
  } catch (error) {
    log("error", "Inline keyboard relay failed: " + (error as Error).message);
  }

  const ids: string[] = [];
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let callbackIndex = 0;

  for (const row of rows) {
    for (const button of row) {
      if (button.url) {
        lines.push("🔗 " + button.text + " ← " + button.url);
      } else {
        ids.push(registerCallback(button.data!, chat, msgId));
        callbackIndex++;
        lines.push(callbackIndex + ". " + button.text + " ← /btn " + callbackIndex);
      }
    }
  }

  state().lastButtonList = ids;

  await tell(text + "\n\n🔘 دکمه‌ها:\n" + lines.join("\n"));
}

/* ---------- peer resolution / channel file fetch / button press ---------- */

/*
  Resolve @username / numeric id (with or without -100 prefix) to
  an input entity, using the dialog list as a fallback cache.
*/
export async function resolvePeer(
  client: TelegramClient,
  target: string
): Promise<unknown> {
  const raw = String(target || "").trim();
  if (!raw) throw new Error("مقصد خالی است.");

  const searchDialogs = async (
    match: (dialog: {
      id: string;
      username: string;
      inputEntity: unknown;
    }) => boolean
  ): Promise<unknown> => {
    const dialogs = await client.getDialogs({ limit: 200 });
    for (const dialog of dialogs) {
      const entity = dialog.entity as unknown as { username?: string };
      const row = {
        id: String(dialog.id),
        username: String(entity?.username || ""),
        inputEntity: dialog.inputEntity,
      };
      if (match(row)) return row.inputEntity;
    }
    return null;
  };

  if (/^-?\d+$/.test(raw)) {
    try {
      return await client.getInputEntity(bigInt(raw));
    } catch {
      const bare = raw.replace(/^-100/, "").replace(/^-/, "");
      const found = await searchDialogs(
        (d) => d.id === bare || d.id === raw
      );
      if (found) return found;
      throw new Error("این شناسه در گفتگوهای حساب پیدا نشد: " + raw);
    }
  }

  const username = raw.replace(/^@/, "");
  try {
    return await client.getInputEntity(username);
  } catch {
    const found = await searchDialogs(
      (d) => d.username.toLowerCase() === username.toLowerCase()
    );
    if (found) return found;
    throw new Error("مقصد پیدا نشد: " + raw);
  }
}

export type ChannelFile = {
  buffer: Buffer;
  name: string;
  mime: string;
  size: number;
  source: string;
  msgId: number;
  joined?: boolean;
};

/* ---------- media analysis (shared by /receive and the probe) ---------- */

/*
  GramJS `Message.file` exposes mimeType/name/size — it has NO `ext`
  property. Extensions must therefore be derived from the mime type
  and the original file name (photos are always JPEG in Telegram).
*/
const MIME_EXTS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  "image/jpeg": [".jpg", ".jpeg"],
  "image/png": [".png"],
  "image/webp": [".webp"],
  "image/gif": [".gif"],
  "image/heic": [".heic", ".heif"],
  "image/tiff": [".tiff", ".tif"],
  "image/bmp": [".bmp"],
  "video/mp4": [".mp4"],
  "video/quicktime": [".mov"],
  "video/x-matroska": [".mkv"],
  "video/webm": [".webm"],
  "video/x-msvideo": [".avi"],
  "video/avi": [".avi"],
  "video/mpeg": [".mpeg", ".mpg"],
  "video/3gpp": [".3gp"],
  "audio/mpeg": [".mp3"],
  "audio/mp3": [".mp3"],
  "audio/mp4": [".m4a"],
  "audio/ogg": [".ogg", ".opus"],
  "audio/opus": [".opus", ".ogg"],
  "audio/flac": [".flac"],
  "audio/x-wav": [".wav"],
  "audio/wav": [".wav"],
  "audio/aac": [".aac"],
  "application/pdf": [".pdf"],
  "application/zip": [".zip"],
  "application/x-zip-compressed": [".zip"],
  "application/vnd.android.package-archive": [".apk"],
  "application/epub+zip": [".epub"],
  "application/msword": [".doc"],
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": [".docx"],
  "application/vnd.ms-excel": [".xls"],
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet": [".xlsx"],
};

/* Extensions that describe the same media kind. */
const EXT_ALIASES: Record<string, string[]> = {
  jpg: ["jpeg", "jfif"],
  jpeg: ["jpg", "jfif"],
  jfif: ["jpg", "jpeg"],
  mpg: ["mpeg"],
  mpeg: ["mpg"],
  tif: ["tiff"],
  tiff: ["tif"],
  htm: ["html"],
  html: ["htm"],
  ogg: ["opus"],
  opus: ["ogg"],
  heic: ["heif"],
  heif: ["heic"],
};

function filenameExt(name: unknown): string {
  const match = /\.([a-z0-9]{1,10})$/i.exec(String(name || ""));
  return match ? "." + match[1].toLowerCase() : "";
}

export type MediaInfo = {
  kind: string;
  mime: string;
  name: string;
  exts: string[];
  size: number;
};

/* Everything /receive needs to know about one message's media. */
export function analyzeMedia(message: Api.Message): MediaInfo | null {
  const photo = message.photo as unknown;
  const document = message.document as unknown;
  if (!photo && !document) return null;

  const file = message.file as unknown as
    | { name?: string; mimeType?: string; size?: unknown }
    | undefined;

  let kind = "document";
  if (photo) kind = "photo";
  else if (message.video) kind = "video";
  else if (message.voice) kind = "voice";
  else if (message.audio) kind = "audio";
  else if (message.sticker) kind = "sticker";
  else if (message.gif) kind = "gif";
  else if (message.videoNote) kind = "videoNote";

  const mime = String(file?.mimeType || (photo ? "image/jpeg" : "")).toLowerCase();
  const name = String(file?.name || "");

  const exts = new Set<string>();
  if (photo) exts.add(".jpg");
  const nameExt = filenameExt(name);
  if (nameExt) exts.add(nameExt);
  for (const e of MIME_EXTS[mime] || []) exts.add(e);

  return {
    kind,
    mime,
    name,
    exts: [...exts],
    size: Number(
      (file?.size as { toString?: () => string })?.toString?.() ??
        file?.size ??
        0
    ),
  };
}

/* .jpg matches .jpeg/.jfif too, .mpg matches .mpeg, ... */
export function extMatches(wanted: string, exts: string[]): boolean {
  const bare = String(wanted || "").replace(/^\./, "").toLowerCase();
  if (!bare) return false;
  if (exts.includes("." + bare)) return true;
  for (const alias of EXT_ALIASES[bare] || []) {
    if (exts.includes("." + alias)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/*
  /receive backend: scan the last 100 messages of a chat and
  download the Nth (x-0 = newest) file matching the extension.
*/
export async function fetchChannelFile(
  target: string,
  ext: string,
  index: number
): Promise<ChannelFile> {
  return telegram(async (client) => {
    const peer = await resolvePeer(client, target);

    let sourceTitle = target;
    try {
      const entity = (await client.getEntity(peer as never)) as unknown as {
        title?: string;
        username?: string;
      };
      sourceTitle =
        entity.title || (entity.username ? "@" + entity.username : target);
    } catch {
      /* keep the raw target as the label */
    }

    let messages = await client.getMessages(peer as never, { limit: 200 });

    /*
      Some public channels hide their history from outsiders and
      expose only a couple of posts. When the history looks truncated
      and the target is a public username, join it once and re-read.
    */
    let joined = false;
    if (messages.length < 15 && !/^-?\d+$/.test(String(target).trim())) {
      try {
        await client.invoke(
          new Api.channels.JoinChannel({
            channel: String(target).trim().replace(/^@/, "") as never,
          })
        );
        joined = true;
        log("info", "Joined " + target + " to read the full history.");
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1500));
        messages = await client.getMessages(peer as never, { limit: 200 });
      } catch (error) {
        const text =
          (error as { errorMessage?: string })?.errorMessage ||
          (error as Error)?.message ||
          "";
        if (!/USER_ALREADY_PARTICIPANT|CHANNEL_PRIVATE|INVITE_HASH/.test(text)) {
          recordError(error);
        }
      }
    }

    const wanted = String(ext || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    const anyFile =
      !wanted || wanted === "*" || wanted === ".*" || wanted === ".any";
    const norm = wanted && !wanted.startsWith(".") ? "." + wanted : wanted;

    const matches: { message: Api.Message; info: MediaInfo }[] = [];
    const extHistogram = new Map<string, number>();
    let mediaCount = 0;

    for (const message of messages) {
      if (!message?.media) continue;

      const info = analyzeMedia(message);
      if (!info) continue;

      mediaCount++;
      for (const e of info.exts) {
        extHistogram.set(e, (extHistogram.get(e) || 0) + 1);
      }

      if (anyFile || extMatches(norm, info.exts)) {
        matches.push({ message, info });
      }
    }

    if (index < 0 || index >= matches.length) {
      if (!messages.length) {
        throw new Error(
          "از " + sourceTitle + " هیچ پیامی خوانده نشد؛ احتمالاً حساب " +
            "به آن دسترسی ندارد یا گفتگو خالی است."
        );
      }

      const histogram = [...extHistogram.entries()]
        .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
        .slice(0, 8)
        .map(([e, n]) => e + "×" + n)
        .join("، ");

      throw new Error(
        "در " + messages.length + " پیام آخرِ " + sourceTitle +
          "، فایل شماره x-" + index + " با پسوند " +
          (norm || "(هر فایلی)") + " نبود." +
          (mediaCount
            ? "\nرسانه‌های موجود: " + mediaCount + " عدد" +
              (histogram ? "\nپسوندها: " + histogram : "") +
              "\nشماره‌های معتبر برای این پسوند: x-0 تا x-" +
              Math.max(matches.length - 1, 0) +
              "؛ برای گرفتن هر رسانه‌ای پسوند را * بگذارید."
            : "\nهیچ فایل یا رسانه‌ای در این بازه نبود.")
      );
    }

    const { message: hit, info } = matches[index];

    if (info.size > MAX_BYTES) {
      throw new Error(
        "فایل هدف " + (info.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) +
          " مگابایت است و از سقف انتقال (" +
          Math.round(MAX_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)) + " مگابایت) بزرگ‌تر است."
      );
    }

    const bytes = await withRetry(
      () =>
        client.downloadMedia(hit, { workers: 1 }) as unknown as Promise<Buffer>,
      2
    );
    if (!bytes) throw new Error("دانلود رسانه ناموفق بود.");

    const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes as unknown as Buffer);
    if (buffer.length > MAX_BYTES) {
      throw new Error("فایل دانلودشده از سقف انتقال بزرگ‌تر است.");
    }

    const name = safeFilename(
      info.name || "tg-" + hit.id + (info.exts[0] || norm || ".bin")
    );

    return {
      buffer,
      name,
      mime: info.mime || "application/octet-stream",
      size: buffer.length,
      source: sourceTitle + " (" + target + ")",
      msgId: hit.id,
      joined,
    };
  });
}

/* Press a Telegram bot inline button on the owner's behalf. */
export async function pressCallback(
  chat: string,
  msgId: number,
  dataB64: string
): Promise<string> {
  return telegram(async (client) => {
    const peer = await resolvePeer(client, chat);

    const answer = await client.invoke(
      new Api.messages.GetBotCallbackAnswer({
        peer: peer as never,
        msgId,
        data: Buffer.from(dataB64, "base64"),
      })
    );

    const response = answer.response
      ? Buffer.from(answer.response as Uint8Array).toString("utf8")
      : "";

    return response || "دکمه فشرده شد ✅";
  });
}

/* ---------- automatic Telegram -> Bale forwarding ---------- */

type SenderInfo = {
  title?: string;
  firstName?: string;
  lastName?: string;
  username?: string;
  bot?: boolean;
};

function senderLabel(sender: SenderInfo | undefined): string {
  if (!sender) return "";
  if (sender.title) return sender.title;

  const name = [sender.firstName, sender.lastName]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ")
    .trim();
  const label = name || (sender.username ? "@" + sender.username : "");

  return label + (sender.bot ? " (ربات)" : "");
}

/*
  Raw update dispatcher. GramJS guarantees this observer fires for
  every update; only the four message-bearing classes matter here.
  out=true (messages sent by this account itself) is ignored, which
  reproduces the previous `NewMessage({ incoming: true })` filter.
*/
async function onRawUpdate(update: unknown): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const u = update as {
      className?: string;
      out?: boolean;
      message?: unknown;
      userId?: unknown;
      chatId?: unknown;
      fromId?: unknown;
      id?: number;
      date?: number;
      message?: string;
      fwdFrom?: unknown;
      viaBotId?: unknown;
      replyTo?: unknown;
      entities?: unknown;
      ttlPeriod?: unknown;
      _entities?: Map<string, unknown>;
    };

    if (process.env.TG_DEBUG === "1" && u.className) {
      log("info", "TG raw: " + u.className);
    }

    if (
      u.className === "UpdateNewMessage" ||
      u.className === "UpdateNewChannelMessage"
    ) {
      const inner = u.message as { className?: string } | undefined;
      if (inner?.className === "Message") {
        void processIncomingMessage(inner as Api.Message).catch(recordError);
      }
      return;
    }

    /*
      UpdateShortMessage / UpdateShortChatMessage arrive for 1:1
      chats and small groups. They carry no Message object, so a
      synthetic one is built exactly like GramJS's own NewMessage
      builder does.
    */
    if (u.className === "UpdateShortMessage" && u.out === false) {
      const synthetic = new Api.Message({
        id: Number(u.id),
        peerId: new Api.PeerUser({ userId: u.userId as never }),
        fromId: new Api.PeerUser({ userId: u.userId as never }),
        message: String(u.message || ""),
        date: Number(u.date),
        fwdFrom: u.fwdFrom as never,
        viaBotId: u.viaBotId as never,
        replyTo: u.replyTo as never,
        entities: (u.entities as never) || [],
        ttlPeriod: u.ttlPeriod as never,
      });
      (synthetic as unknown as { _entities: Map<string, unknown> })._entities =
        u._entities || new Map();

      void processIncomingMessage(synthetic).catch(recordError);
      return;
    }

    if (u.className === "UpdateShortChatMessage" && u.out === false) {
      const synthetic = new Api.Message({
        id: Number(u.id),
        peerId: new Api.PeerChat({ chatId: u.chatId as never }),
        fromId: new Api.PeerUser({ userId: u.fromId as never }),
        message: String(u.message || ""),
        date: Number(u.date),
        entities: (u.entities as never) || [],
        ttlPeriod: u.ttlPeriod as never,
      });
      (synthetic as unknown as { _entities: Map<string, unknown> })._entities =
        u._entities || new Map();

      void processIncomingMessage(synthetic).catch(recordError);
      return;
    }
  } catch (error) {
    recordError(error);
  }
}

async function processIncomingMessage(event: unknown): Promise<void> {
  {
    const message = event as Api.Message;
    if (!message?.peerId) return;

    const s = state();
    const settings = s.settings;
    const runtime = s.runtime;

    if (!settings.ownerId || runtime.paused) return;

    const chatId = String(utils.getPeerId(message.peerId));
    const isPrivate = message.peerId instanceof Api.PeerUser;

    /*
      Diagnostics: one line per incoming Telegram message so the
      event pipeline (and the allow gate) is always observable in
      the log.
    */
    log(
      "info",
      "TG event: chat=" + chatId +
        (isPrivate ? " private" : " chat") +
        " msg=" + message.id +
        " media=" + (message.media ? "1" : "0") +
        " allowed=" +
        (
          (settings.autoAll && isPrivate) ||
          settings.watch.includes("*") ||
          settings.watch.includes(chatId)
            ? "1"
            : "0"
        )
    );

    /*
      autoAll: EVERY private chat (people AND bots) is mirrored to
      the Bale owner with zero configuration. The watch list still
      adds groups/channels on top (or * for literally everything).
    */
    const allowed =
      (settings.autoAll && isPrivate) ||
      settings.watch.includes("*") ||
      settings.watch.includes(chatId);

    if (!allowed) return;

    let sender = message.sender as unknown as SenderInfo | undefined;
    if (!sender) {
      try {
        sender = (await message.getSender()) as unknown as
          | SenderInfo
          | undefined;
      } catch {
        /* label falls back to unknown */
      }
    }
    const fromLabel = senderLabel(sender) || "ناشناس";

    const header =
      "📥 تلگرام | از: " + fromLabel +
      " | شناسه فرستنده: " +
      (message.senderId ? String(message.senderId) : "-") +
      " | چت: " + chatId +
      (isPrivate ? "" : " | پیام: " + message.id);

    const file = message.file as unknown as
      | {
          size?: unknown;
          name?: string;
          ext?: string;
          mimeType?: string;
        }
      | undefined;

    const rows = extractRelayRows(message.replyMarkup);
    const body = String(message.message || "");

    try {
      if (settings.receiveText && body) {
        await relayWithButtons(
          header + "\n\n" + body,
          rows,
          chatId,
          message.id
        );
        log(
          "info",
          "TG->Bale text relayed: chat=" + chatId + " msg=" + message.id
        );
      } else if (settings.receiveText && message.media && !file) {
        await relayWithButtons(
          header +
            "\n\nاین نوع رسانه قابل انتقال فایل نیست (نظرسنجی، موقعیت و ...).",
          rows,
          chatId,
          message.id
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      recordError(error);
      await tell(
        header + "\n\nخطا در ارسال متن به بله: " +
          ((error as Error)?.message || String(error))
      ).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (!settings.receiveFiles || !file) return;

    const declared = Number(
      (file.size as { toString?: () => string })?.toString?.() ??
        file.size ??
        0
    );

    if (declared > MAX_BYTES) {
      await tell(
        header +
          "\n\nحجم رسانه " + (declared / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) +
          " مگابایت است؛ سقف انتقال " +
          Math.round(MAX_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)) +
          " مگابایت — منتقل نشد."
      );
      return;
    }

    const name = safeFilename(
      file.name ||
        "telegram-" + message.id + (analyzeMedia(message)?.exts[0] || ".bin")
    );

    let bytes: unknown;
    try {
      bytes = await withRetry(
        () =>
          telegram((client) =>
            client.downloadMedia(message, { workers: 1 })
          ) as unknown as Promise<Buffer>,
        2
      );
    } catch (error) {
      await tell(
        header +
          "\n\nخطا در دانلود رسانه از تلگرام: " + recordError(error)
      ).catch(() => {});
      return;
    }
    if (!bytes) return;

    const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes as unknown as Buffer);
    if (buffer.length > MAX_BYTES) {
      await tell(header + "\n\nرسانه پس از دانلود از سقف حجم بزرگ‌تر شد.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      await withRetry(
        () =>
          bot(
            "bale",
            "sendDocument",
            {
              chat_id: settings.ownerId,
              caption: Array.from(
                header + (body ? "\n\n" + body : "")
              ).slice(0, 900).join(""),
            },
            {
              buffer,
              name,
              mime: file.mimeType || "application/octet-stream",
            }
          ),
        2
      );
      log(
        "info",
        "TG->Bale file relayed: chat=" + chatId +
          " msg=" + message.id + " name=" + name
      );
    } catch (error) {
      await tell(
        header + "\n\nخطا در ارسال فایل به بله: " + recordError(error)
      ).catch(() => {});
      return;
    }

    if (rows.length) {
      await relayWithButtons(
        header + "\n🔘 دکمه‌های همراه فایل:",
        rows,
        chatId,
        message.id
      );
    }
  }
}

import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

const globalForPrisma = globalThis as unknown as {
  prisma: PrismaClient | undefined
}

export const db =
  globalForPrisma.prisma ??
  new PrismaClient({
    log: ['query'],
  })

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') globalForPrisma.prisma = db

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}
